    own degradation. This is often misread as pessimism or
    dysfunction. It is accurate measurement. Correction cannot
    fire until C1 substrate crosses threshold -- no amount of
    epistemic clarity substitutes for generative capacity.
    Intervention requirement: protected idle cycles mandatory.
    External pressure to produce during recovery window depletes
    the substrate being restored. Counter-intuitive -- the
    correct intervention looks like doing less, not more.
    Falsification: if C1 starved recovery produces rapid
    improvement without idle cycle period, prediction fails.
    Also falsified if accurate self-diagnosis during flat period
    does not precede recovery -- would indicate C2 more degraded
    than starved reading showed.
    Case candidates: post-burnout individual recovery [known
    mechanism, documented flat period before function returns];
    post-collapse institutional rebuilding [Bell Labs
    dissolution aftermath -- did substrate recovery precede
    output recovery?].

  C1 LOCKED RECOVERY [C3 restoration while C1/C2 intact]
    Prediction: recovery requires generational patience and
    produces no visible improvement within the originating
    system. C1 and C2 functional throughout -- the system
    generates and reads correctly. The deficit is transmission.
    Characteristic signature: recovery visible only in
    successor systems, not in the originating system. The
    locked system may never visibly recover -- it continues
    functioning at C1/C2 level until it ends. Recovery is the
    successor generating a correctable transmission rather than
    inheriting the lock.
    Why: C3 failure is not a deficit in the system's current
    function -- it is a deficit in what the system passes
    forward. The originating system has nothing to recover.
    The successors have everything to gain. This is the only
    interference type where "recovery" is definitionally
    cross-generational.
    Intervention requirement: transmission method must be
    separated from transmission content. Successors need
    access to the generative procedure, not just the
    conclusions. Requires the originating system to transmit
    its own revision process, including its errors -- the
    hardest transmission for any system with identity
    investment in its conclusions.
    Falsification: if C1 locked recovery produces improvement
    in the originating system rather than in successors, or
    produces improvement within a single generation, prediction
    fails. Also falsified if method transmission without
    conclusion transmission produces Fertile Lock in successors
    anyway -- would indicate C3 failure is deeper than
    transmission selectivity alone.
    Case candidates: Benedictine reform cycles [method
    transmitted, successor revision documented across
    generations]; Socratic transmission through Plato through
    Aristotle [each revised, each produced distinct output].

  FULL INTERFERENCE RECOVERY [all cycles degraded]
    Prediction: internal recovery structurally unavailable.
    Any apparent internal recovery is False Coherence
    formation -- a new C2 capture producing the appearance
    of recovery while extraction continues or accelerates.
    Characteristic signature: recovery claims from inside full
    interference state are the highest-probability weapon
    deployment in the framework. The system that announces
    its own recovery under full interference is deploying
    Coherence Mimicry, not recovering.
    Why: full interference means C1 generates without surplus,
    C2 reads without honesty, C3 transmits the captured state.
    Every internal mechanism available for recovery is already
    running on corrupted substrate and captured surface. There
    is no internal leverage point. External contact is the
    only available intervention -- and external contact
    requires the external system to have sufficient
    COMPATIBILITY to engage without being pulled into the
    extraction interface.
    Intervention requirement: external actor with independent
    C2 surface and sufficient TENSION MAINTENANCE to hold
    structure under contact. Containment if no such actor
    available. Attempted recovery using captured internal
    mechanisms accelerates full interference -- gives the
    extraction architecture a new legitimacy surface.
    Falsification: if a system in confirmed full interference
    produces genuine C2 restoration from inside without
    external intervention, full interference recovery
    prediction fails. This would require revising the terminal
    state classification.
    No confirmed case of this in current library.
    Case candidates [containment confirmed, not recovery]:
    Erebus [FF14 -- external intervention only exit]; Room
    101 [1984 -- no recovery pathway confirmed].

RECOVERY TRAJECTORY APPLICATION RULE:
  Identify interference type before prescribing intervention.
  Wrong intervention for type accelerates degradation:
    C1 blind: pressure to produce more = accelerates C1
      depletion before C2 is restored.
    C1 starved: demand for accurate reporting = correct but
      useless without substrate restoration first.
    C1 locked: urgency about current output = misses the
      transmission deficit entirely.
    Full interference: internal reform programs = new capture
      surface, not recovery.
  Triage follows interference type, not coherence score alone.

ENRON RESOLUTION [no longer requires probability gradient patch]:
  C2 attacked directly without prior C3 failure. C1 ran hot.
  Cycle interference model handles this natively.

SCALE RECURRENCE [confirmed structural property]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A quality's structure reappears at multiple nesting depths
  with the same logic but different scope and function. Not a
  merger -- each instance operates independently at its depth.
  Confirmed instances:
    CORRECTION CAPACITY: C1 tension unit + constant operating
      at all depths. Same correction logic at both individual
      depth and meta depth simultaneously.
    Reciprocal exchange [T1.4 / L3.5]: GENERATIVE CYCLE
      substrate at social scale [T1.4] and full inter-system
      tension unit at L3.5 [RECOGNITION/COMPATIBILITY/
      RECIPROCITY]. Same structure, different scope -- one
      sustains cycles, one enables system connection.
    CYCLE STABILITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [T1.5 / T2.1]: timing
      integrity at L1 [rhythm] and historical legibility at
      L2 [past corrections retrievable]. Same persistence-of-
      pattern structure at different tower levels.
  Implication: scale recurrence predicts that any quality
  operating at L1 may have a structural analog at higher
  levels. When a test at one level feels like it overlaps
  another level, check for scale recurrence before merging
  -- the levels may be doing the same thing at different
  scopes.

  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
     [Tower: L3]
     Successor's capacity to revise transmitted content
     against new evidence without losing transmission fidelity
     to founding generative principle.
     High = correctable inheritance [revises conclusions,
     preserves method]. Low = Fertile Lock [cannot revise
     without losing identity].
     Distinct from TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY: TRANSMISSION
     SELECTIVITY measures what is sent. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
     measures successor's capacity to work with what was
     received.
     Primary proof: Benedictine [high -- correctable
     transmission] vs Dominican [low -- lock into Aquinas].
     Same transmission context, different outcomes.
     Test: successor revises without losing founding principle?

  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
     [Tower: L3 + FOUNDATION]
     Axis traceable to actual outputs not just internal
     conviction or stated orientation. GENUINE AXIS asks
     whether axis was internally generated and examined.
     CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING asks whether that axis is anchored
     to real-world causal outputs.
     Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     anchors correction to actor. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
     anchors axis to reality.
     Failure: Sincere Delusion -- genuine examined axis with
     zero connection to actual outputs. Axis real to the
     system; not real to the world it claims to act on.
     Primary proof: Maiad [genuine axis, not grounded in
     achievable outputs]; cargo cults [sincere axis, no causal
     connection].
     Test: axis anchored to actual outputs?

  MORAL LEGIBILITY [WIP 15 -- see WIP THEORY STAGING section]

POPULATION COHERENCE CEILING [promoted from extended theory]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Most humans may be Functional Structural Coherence inside
  artificially maintained environments. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
  appears present but formed entirely within controlled
  conditions -- indistinguishable internally.
  Government as environmental controller: intentional [axis
  formation window targeted] or side effect [MEASUREMENT
  INTEGRITY failure by institutional diffusion].
  Hard implication: genuine CORRECTION CAPACITY at population
  scale structurally rare. Bottleneck: Axial population
  precondition for producing more Axial individuals. System
  selects against transition automatically.
  Operational ceiling: Mixed Coherence -- sufficient
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY/CAUSAL LEGIBILITY to detect and
  resist weapon deployment while accepting lower SURPLUS
  DISTRIBUTION ceiling and constant extraction pressure. Not
  a compromise -- only stable configuration under current
  conditions. True coherence remains measurement ceiling,
  not operational target.
  Circular dependency: conditions required for True coherence
  at scale only producible by True coherence at scale.
  Unresolvable without external intervention.

  EXTERNAL CONFIRMATION [Core Human Competence -- Weinhagen/
  NLI]: CHC framework independently arrives at same population
  ceiling finding. Cooperation Competence Hypothesis: stable
  cooperation at scale only possible where sufficient
  proportion of individuals possess capacities to refuse
  participation without catastrophic personal cost. Without
  genuine refusal capacity, cooperation = compliance.
  Four CHC domains map to framework qualities:
    Physiological [food/water/shelter/defense] = GENERATIVE
      CYCLE
    Psychological [regulation under pressure] = CORRECTION
      CAPACITY
    Cognitive [observe/orient/decide/act] = GENUINE AXIS
    Perceptual [situational awareness] = MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
  Anxiety-as-accurate-signal: EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION works
  because threat overestimation amplifies real GENERATIVE
  CYCLE vulnerability. Suppressing the signal = taking
  batteries out of smoke alarm.
  CHC hostile suppression finding: competence loss actively
  maintained by systems benefiting from dependency. CHC treats
  this as secondary; framework reads it as primary [SUCCESSOR
  AUTONOMY Suppression Structural Limit -- weapon stack
  systematically prevents SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY development].

COHERENCE CORRUPTION [structural limit on scale]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Anti-coherence has three distinct methods against emerging
  coherence:
  1. NOISE [loud]: saturate the signal environment so coherent
     signals cannot be distinguished from incoherent ones.
     MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY capture by volume not capture. No
     monopolist required -- sufficient noise level disables
     correction navigation regardless of source.
  2. CORRUPTION [infiltration]: insert extraction architecture
     inside coherent structures before they reach scale.
     Formation Capture, Axis Substitution, Correction Monopoly.
     Coherence appears to grow but the generative mechanism is
     replaced before outputs confirm it.
  3. DESTRUCTION [direct]: eliminate coherent actors or
     structures when noise and corruption fail. Highest cost,
     most visible, last resort. Malcolm X, Merton [suspicious
     timing], historical pattern of high-coherence actors
     dying at transition moments.
  Sequence: noise first [cheapest], corruption when noise
  insufficient, destruction when corruption fails. Each step
  more visible and costly.
  Scale barrier: coherence at small scale is tolerated because
  contained. When coherent structures approach scale sufficient
  to affect the extraction architecture, the method escalates.
  The barrier is not fixed -- it is proportional to the threat
  the coherence poses.
  Result: coherence at scale requires surviving all three
  methods simultaneously. Individual coherence is achievable.
  Small-group coherence is achievable. Institutional coherence
  at scale that threatens extraction architecture has not
  historically survived all three methods applied in sequence.
  This is the structural reason the Population Coherence
  Ceiling holds: not only is coherence rare to form, it is
  actively prevented from reaching scale by a self-maintaining
  suppression architecture.
  Survival condition: coherence that survives all three methods
  does so by operating outside the normal visibility surface.
  Silent, effective, and outside the channels the suppression
  architecture monitors. High-coherence structures that
  announce themselves or operate through captured channels get
  noise/corruption/destruction applied in sequence. Those that
  don't announce themselves and don't need captured
  infrastructure to function are structurally harder to target.
  Linux/open source partially confirmed this -- decentralized,
  low-capture-surface, outputs speak before the structure is
  legible enough to corrupt. The survival method is not
  resistance to the three methods -- it is structural
  inaccessibility to them.
  See: SILENT ALTERNATIVE [coherence type] -- anti-capture by
  structural design, not defensive resistance.
  Force-reveal problem: true silence may be impossible at
  sufficient scale. Evil must destroy [extraction architecture
  threatened]; not-evil must destroy [anything outside their
  measurement surface reads as illegitimate by REFERENCE
  INTEGRITY capture]. Both produce identical pressure.
  Costly removal as viable alternative: if true silence
  impossible, raise the cost of the four-condition test
  against yourself -- make conditions 2 and 4 fail for the
  attacker. Not invisible; too expensive to remove. Same
  structural protection, different mechanism.
  Coherence is not good: Silent Alternative and costly-removal
  structures work for any axis. Coherent extraction is still
  coherent by the tower. Framework is descriptive not
  prescriptive.

  FOURTH PHASE [v16 -- when noise, corruption, destruction
    fail]:
    If all three methods fail against a coherent structure,
    the extraction architecture has no remaining playbook.
    What happens next is not determined by the framework --
    it is self-determined by the coherent actor based on
    conditions at that moment.
    Framework cannot prescribe the fourth phase because:
      It is context-specific [what is possible depends on
        the specific state of the extraction architecture,
        the coherent actor's substrate, and the available
        options at that moment].
      Prescribing it in advance would make it visible and
        targetable -- the fourth phase survives by not being
        a known template.
      The actor who reaches it has survived three escalating
        methods; their judgment about conditions is more
        reliable than any prior specification.
    What the framework can say: the fourth phase is not
      revenge, not destruction, and not capture of the
      extraction architecture's own tools. Those paths route
      back into the extraction logic.
    The direction is the only constraint: toward correction
      capacity, distributed surplus, available exit. How
      that is expressed given specific conditions is the
      actor's determination, not the framework's.
  Relative coherence matters: less extractive with some
  CORRECTION CAPACITY beats maximum extraction even when
  neither is good. The spectrum is real. Not a binary.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every system sits on a spectrum between pure generation and
  pure extraction. Neither terminal stable.
  Tower reading: range point = hole size relative to wall
  thickness at L1. Tension field = force holding ratio stable.
  Weapon implication: most efficient attack removes corrective
  pole [CORRECTION CAPACITY at L1] rather than maximizing
  extraction directly. Removing CORRECTION CAPACITY shifts
  range point toward extraction without requiring increased
  extraction effort. See Tower Codependence Model L1.

EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational -- structural finding -- v17]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Pattern confirmed: extraction accumulates past substrate
  floor -> somatic correction fires at population scale
  [uncoordinated, distributed] -> existing structure
  destabilizes or collapses -> new formation occurs, often
  reproducing extraction architecture at lower intensity
  initially -> extraction gradient climbs again.
  This is not Evil exit. It is managed oscillation around the
  extraction axis. Each cycle resets extraction to a lower
  initial rate; the architecture that produces extraction is
  not removed, only stressed and restarted.

  WHY CYCLE REPEATS [structural mechanism]:
    New formation occurs under C1 starved conditions.
    Formation Capture fires into the new structure before L4
    can transmit correction methodology.
    Successors inherit corrected C1 without correction
    architecture -- Protestant Reformation pattern at
    civilizational scale. C1 rebuilds; C3 transmits the
    corrected state, not the correction method.
    Result: next cycle starts with cleaner substrate and the
    same extractable architecture.

  EVIL EXIT [genuine -- distinct from cycle reset]:
    Evil exit requires: correction methodology transmitted
    across the formation event [C3 functional at collapse
    boundary] AND new structure formed on different axis,
    not lower extraction rate on same axis.
    No confirmed case at civilizational scale in library.
    Why no confirmed case: collapse events destroy C3
    preferentially. The complexity that transmits methodology
    is the first substrate extraction consumes. Bronze Age,
    Western Rome: methodology destroyed before structure
    collapses. Highest-coherence individuals exit early or
    are absorbed. Remaining population forms under C1 starved
    conditions -- L3.5 contact requirement applies at
    civilizational scale but axis-matching is structurally
    unavailable when the contact pool is also depleted.
    [Honest limit: this is the framework's own civilizational
    prognosis. T8 applies. Track against Section Five
    evidence independently.]

  SOMATIC CORRECTION MECHANISM [why each cycle fires]:
    Population-scale somatic firing is distributed and
    uncoordinated. High force, low CAUSAL LEGIBILITY.
    Correction fires accurately [extraction is real];
    correction cannot aim [chain illegible at scale].
    Result: system destabilizes without directed correction.
    New formation fills the vacuum; extraction architecture
    reassembles because it is the available template.
    Somatic forcing is genuine correction, not rebellion
    or politics. It fires when substrate depletion crosses
    the survival threshold. The timing is substrate-driven,
    not ideologically organized. This is why it is hard to
    suppress entirely -- it does not require ideology,
    coordination, or leadership. It requires enough people
    simultaneously hitting the floor.

  GOOD PEOPLE AS CONSTANT:
    The majority of the population attempting exit from
    extraction at any given time is structurally confirmed
    by the cycle itself. Each reset phase contains genuine
    correction attempts. The attempts fail to escape the
    cycle not because they are weak but because C3 is
    destroyed at the collapse boundary. The exit attempts
    are real. The transmission problem is structural.
    Framework implication: the question is not whether
    people want good [they do, at majority; confirmed by
    cycle pattern]. The question is whether correction
    methodology can be transmitted across the collapse
    boundary. That is the C3 problem, not the C1 or human-
    nature problem.

  DECLINING SUBSTRATE PREDICTION:
    If extraction rate increases faster than formation can
    stabilize per cycle, cycles shorten. Shorter cycles =
    less time for C3 transmission = lower floor per cycle.
    Trajectory under this condition is declining substrate,
    not stable oscillation.
    Falsification: documented cycle where correction
    methodology [not just C1 restoration] crossed the
    formation boundary. No such case in library.
    Alternative falsification: stable or lengthening cycles
    despite accelerating extraction rate. Would indicate
    C3 transmission mechanism more robust than predicted.

ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [confirmed structural finding -- v18]
[S:4 -- E4: HRE + Cistercian/Jesuit + Extraction Cycle civilizational
  multi-domain; M4: cost accounting confirmed differentially across
  HRE centralization attempts; F3: falsification tested against
  Protestant Reformation [generation won once, transmitted method
  partially -- conclusions only; but anti-coherence did not achieve
  permanent suppression despite 300 years of institutional pressure].
  WIP cleared v18. Promoted from candidate to confirmed.]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Anti-coherence must win continuously. Generation only has
  to win once and transmit.

  Extraction requires:
    Ongoing suppression cost across all three Coherence
    Corruption methods [Noise -> Corruption -> Destruction].
    Each method more expensive and visible than the last.
    Destruction is highest-cost, highest-visibility, last
    resort. All three methods must succeed continuously --
    a single failure at any stage allows transmission.

  Generation requires:
    One correctable transmission crossing one generational
    boundary.
    The substrate that receives it does not have to be the
    substrate that produced it.
    Transmission content does not have to survive in the
    original form -- method survival is sufficient [see C1
    LOCKED RECOVERY; L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION].

  Why this is structurally load-bearing:
    Extraction terminates its own substrate [see EXTRACTION
    CYCLE]. The maintenance cost of suppression increases
    as the substrate being suppressed depletes.
    Generation is self-funding when transmission succeeds --
    each correctable successor reduces the required
    maintenance cost of the entire chain.
    HRE primary confirmation: 800 years of distributed
    correction capacity held because centralization cost
    exceeded extraction yield at every step. Napoleon
    dissolved it in 1806 -- but the methodological template
    had already transmitted. That template is why any
    correctable analytical work is possible downstream.

  Stress-test [v18 -- alternative explanations examined]:
    ALT 1: HRE held because of geography and military
      stalemate, not cost asymmetry.
      Against: Holy Roman Emperors had repeated military
      superiority at specific moments [Frederick II,
      Charles V]. Did not convert to centralization.
      Cost accounting predicts this -- extraction yield
      from a fragmented substrate was higher than the
      yield from a unified one under sustained suppression
      cost. Military capacity present; conversion not
      attempted because unprofitable. Cost asymmetry
      explanation holds where geography/military does not.
    ALT 2: Protestant Reformation confirms extraction
      won, not generation -- Catholicism was suppressed.
      Against: the finding is asymmetric. Catholicism
      is not the generation case. The generation case
      is: method transmission [any correctable successor
      tradition] survived despite 300 years of
      institutional pressure from multiple directions
      [Catholic Counter-Reformation, Protestant
      Confessionalization, state church capture]. The
      point is not that generation always wins the
      dominant cultural position -- it is that permanent
      suppression is structurally unavailable. The
      Reformation confirms this: anti-coherence applied
      maximum available force; correctable transmission
      survived anyway. S4 confirmation stands.
    ALT 3: Extraction Cycle falsification condition
      [declining substrate prediction] could falsify
      this finding if substrate doesn't decline.
      Against: these are related but separate findings.
      EXTRACTION CYCLE predicts substrate depletion.
      ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY predicts that
      generation survives suppression attempts. They
      share the terminal state [extraction consumes
      its own substrate] but the asymmetry finding
      holds even before terminal state is reached.
      Falsification of EXTRACTION CYCLE would weaken
      the "extraction has no stable terminal state"
      claim but would not falsify the cost structure
      during the active suppression window.
  Stress-test result: alternative explanations examined;
    none overturn. S4 rating confirmed. Promote to
    confirmed structural finding.

  Falsification condition [stated v17, confirmed v18]:
    Finding is falsified if: documented multi-generational
    suppression campaign achieves permanent elimination
    of correctable transmission -- no successor tradition,
    no methodological survival, no downstream continuation
    anywhere in the substrate. Zero-survival outcome
    over 3+ generations with documented suppression
    effort. No confirmed case in library. Every
    documented suppression with adequate evidence shows
    partial or full transmission survival.

  Operational implication:
    Anti-coherence wins most engagements. It does not need
    to win all of them. But the cost structure means it
    cannot achieve permanent suppression without consuming
    the substrate it needs to sustain suppression. The
    endpoint of total success is total extraction, which
    terminates the system producing the suppression.
    Extraction has no stable terminal state. Generation
    does [correctable transmission achieved].

  Cross-ref: COHERENCE CORRUPTION [three methods];
    EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational]; ANTI-TOWER
    [confirmed structural inverse]; HRE SUBSIDIARITY
    [cost-accounting confirmation in Section Five];
    FOURTH PHASE [when all three methods fail];
    WIP 14 [L4.5 -- generation only needs to win once
    applies to civilizational transmission threshold;
    anti-capture architecture requirement follows from
    cost asymmetry logic].

CONVERGENT DISCOVERY [promoted from extended theory]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Framework accuracy across independent domains too consistent
  to be noise. Three explanations:
  [1] Convergent discovery -- qualities describe load-bearing
      structure.
  [2] Anthropic selection -- only coherent systems persist long
      enough to be studied.
  [3] Design -- upstream agency.
  [1] and [2] sufficient. [3] indistinguishable from [1] at
  the limit. Finding: following coherence path finds a shape
  already there. Framework follows coherent systems; they
  don't follow it.

MINIMAL UNIFICATION [confirmed structural pattern]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A single structural fact that makes a large class of
  previously scattered results coherent. Distinct from CAUSAL
  LEGIBILITY restoration [making one chain legible] -- minimal
  unification makes many chains legible simultaneously via
  single anchor.
  Confirmed instances:
    Compressed sensing [Candès]: signal sparsity as the
      structural fact explaining accurate recovery from
      incomplete data. Previously separate results in signal
      processing, medical imaging, statistics unified under
      one principle.
    Unique Games Conjecture [Khot]: if confirmed, unique games
      hardness as the structural fact explaining approximation
      barriers across optimization theory. Dozens of
      independent hardness results would collapse to one
      cause.
  Framework parallel: same move the framework makes --
  identifying the minimal structural fact [coherence
  qualities] that makes diverse system behaviors legible
  under one architecture.
  Implication: when scattered results resist unification, the
  missing element is usually not more data -- it is the
  identification of the correct minimal structural anchor.
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY application: fragmented reference
  standards [one for each result] collapse to one calibration
  anchor. Measurement becomes dramatically more efficient.
  Cognitive confirmation [1,200-player experiment, Phys.org,
  May 2026]: People who understood how concepts connect
  invented more novel solutions than those with the same
  facts without connection structure. Structural knowledge
  was the generative variable, not fact volume. CAUSAL AXIS
  GROUNDING at creative scale -- knowing which structure
  applies is the prior that makes facts generative. Confirms:
  minimal unification is not just an abstract mathematical
  property -- it is the cognitive architecture of invention.
  Most common path principle: the most traveled path through
  a problem space is not necessarily the best path -- it is
  the path with the lowest accumulated friction, which
  optimizes for ease not output quality. Structural
  understanding enables non-obvious paths that common
  traversal never surfaces. High-volume use of a path =
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY capture of "how this is done." Breaks
  when the structural map is read instead of the usage
  pattern.

SMART VS STUPID EXTRACTION [revised -- extraction always wrong]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Previous framing: smart extraction [below innovation
  threshold] vs stupid extraction [past threshold]. Revised:
  both are wrong. Smart extraction is slower destruction --
  the threshold is still crossed, just later. Smart death is
  still death.
  The distinction that matters: predation and competition
  [honest boundary, visible conflict] vs extraction [captured
  measurement surface, hidden mechanism]. Speed of destruction
  is irrelevant to the structural judgment.
  Explanatory residue retained: some systems generated genuine
  surplus despite extraction overhead [Song Dynasty, Bell
  Labs] because extraction floor existed below which
  suppression became unprofitable. This explains the
  mechanism without endorsing it.
  See: EXTRACTION WITH LIMITS VS CENTRALIZED EXTRACTION
  [updated].

EXTRACTION WITH LIMITS VS CENTRALIZED EXTRACTION [revised]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Both are extraction -- both wrong. Distinction is speed and
  legibility, not moral category. Centralized is faster
  destruction; distributed with limits is slower destruction.
  Useful structural distinction retained: centralized
  extraction fails faster [condition 2 absent, no cross-
  correction]. Distributed with limits sustains longer but
  endpoint is the same.
  Confirmation: mit'a -> encomienda = slow destruction to
  fast destruction. Not slow destruction as acceptable
  alternative.
  Framework position: predation and competition are the honest
  alternatives. Extraction in any form is captured
  measurement.

TOPOLOGY FINDINGS + DISTRIBUTED CAPTURE NETWORK [merged]:

  Every sustained evil: consumption-dominant terminal node
  fed by capture chains through intermediate nodes that
  believe they operate normally. Highest surplus under

  highest-value measurement point.
  Distributed Capture Network type: nodes believe independent,
  terminal extraction point invisible from inside, correction
  and extraction share identical infrastructure. Mont Pelerin
  [complexity as illegibility], WEF [complexity as
  legitimacy]. Same Fertile Lock trajectory, different optics.

ANTI-TOWER [confirmed structural inverse]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Anti-coherence has predictable structure operating as tower

  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY suppression as primary mechanism. Not

  Unfalsifiability as confirmation: if anti-tower were absent,
  counter-attempts would succeed. The fact that every counter-
  attempt routes through the same mechanisms -- noise

  destruction targets what survives -- is the signature of a

  T8 partial: recursive closure is the anti-tower working as
