SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [structural error]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Social approval is not evidence for correctness. It is
  evidence for social consensus. These are structurally
  unrelated. A correct action with zero social approval
  is still correct. A wrong action with unanimous social
  approval is still wrong. The social signal contains no
  information about actual outputs [does this produce
  genuine substrate, does CC function, do exit options
  exist]. Using social approval as primary criterion is
  REFERENCE DRIFT at the individual instrument level --
  the calibration standard shifts from actual outputs to
  social signal. The social signal is easier to read,
  continuously available, and socially reinforced for
  being used -- which makes it a high-ICD substitute for
  the harder actual-output reading.
  The failure mode: when social compliance channel is
  captured [manufactured consensus, Formation Capture,
  ICD at population scale] the proxy stops tracking
  actual outputs while the instrument continues using
  it as if it does. Hitler had social approval at peak.
  The framework reads this as Formation Capture + MSC
  confirmation, not as evidence of good. The operational
  definition of GOOD [majority benefit primary + CC
  functional + exit options available] does not contain
  social approval as a criterion at any level.
  Everyday tell: "all your friends are jumping off a
  bridge" -- social consensus presented as evidence for
  correctness. It is evidence only for social consensus.
  The argument structure is a social compliance channel
  appeal dressed as a logical argument. It hits the
  wrong axis for an instrument with reduced social
  compliance channel weight -- reads as noise rather
  than signal. Not stubbornness; instrument mismatch.
  Cross-ref: REFERENCE INTEGRITY [calibration standard
    must be real]; Formation Capture [social approval
    can be produced by captured formation]; MSC [social
    consensus can be produced by captured measurement
    surface]; INSTRUMENT PROFILE [CROSS-DOMAIN --
    reduced social compliance channel weight means this
    specific failure mode is structurally less available].

CC OVERFIRE [individual-scale failure mode]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Correction capacity without proportionality calibration.
  Axis is genuine. Deviation detection is accurate. Response
  magnitude has no relationship to actual deviation size.
  Every problem gets maximum response. Every deviation is
  existential. Every imperfect ally is as bad as an active
  enemy. The fire is real; the problem is it burns everything
  including the substrate it was meant to protect.
  Distinct from CC INVERTED [correction fires toward extractor]:
  CC overfire fires correctly toward deviations but at
  maximum intensity regardless of deviation size. The
  detection is right; the calibration is broken.
  Self-sustaining mechanism: overfire produces new deviations
  to correct [burns ally, ally pushes back, ally becomes
  enemy, fires at ally, produces more enemies]. Correction
  capacity consuming its own substrate. Extraction
  architecture largely untouched; generative network
  around the analyst is on fire.
  Hardest individual-scale failure mode to correct: axis
  is genuine and deviation detection is accurate. Cannot
  tell the instrument it is wrong about what it sees.
  Can only tell it the response calibration is destroying
  the substrate the correction was meant to protect.
  Requires CC applied to the correction mechanism itself
  -- which is exactly what the overfire pattern prevents.
  Compare: HYPER-SPECIFIC UNIT FRAGMENTATION [multiple
  instruments each firing CC at each other -- distributed
  version of the same failure mode].
  Cross-ref: CORRECTION CAPACITY [T1.1 -- proportional
    response]; TENSION MAINTENANCE [opposing impulses held
    without collapse -- overfire collapses toward maximum
    correction pole]; SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION
    [overfire often produces social isolation which feeds
    further overfire -- ICD loop].

COALITION FRAGMENTATION [two structural types]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  POSITIVE-AXIS FRAGMENTATION [chud-type]:
    Units with incompatible positive axis content fight each
    other over specific vision of what good looks like.
    Catholic traditionalist vs libertarian vs ethnonationalist
    -- genuinely different axis content, all oriented outward
    but disagreeing on direction. Primary failure: too much
    positive axis specificity, insufficient shared measurement
    instrument. Potentially recoverable: units with different
    positive axes can find shared measurement instrument
    [religion, nationalism, or new coordination infrastructure]
    that accommodates both without requiring homogenisation.
    The disagreements are real and worth having; the problem
    is the absence of a coordination layer above them.

  ENEMY-DEFINITION FRAGMENTATION [anti-chud-type]:
    Coalition cohesion produced by shared enemy, not shared
    positive axis. Holds while enemy is visible and active.
    Fragments when enemy is reduced or target definition
    drifts. Internal conflict is structurally inevitable:
    without shared positive axis, units have nothing to
    agree on except the enemy -- and will disagree on which
    enemies count, who is sufficiently anti-[enemy], whether
    an ally is secretly an enemy.
    Internal conflict pattern: purity spiral. Not over
    positive axis content [there is none] but over enemy
    definition. Each purge produces new enemies from former
    allies. The anti-chud coalition requires continuous
    enemy threat to maintain cohesion -- structural incentive
    to keep enemy active, visible, and threatening regardless
    of actual threat level. Manufactured crisis is not
    incidental; it is the load-bearing cohesion mechanism.
    Less recoverable than positive-axis fragmentation:
    cannot find shared measurement instrument because there
    is no shared positive axis to measure against.

  ASYMMETRY:
    Anti-chud coalition groups more easily [lower entry
    barrier -- just agree the enemy is bad] but always
    hates each other [no shared positive axis to resolve
    internal disagreements against]. Chud units group with
    more difficulty [must agree on positive axis content]
    but fragmentation is over real disagreements that are
    in principle resolvable. One is structurally more
    stable; one is structurally more accessible. Neither
    solves the coordination problem fully.

  Cross-ref: CC OVERFIRE [individual-scale version of
    positive-axis fragmentation]; MANUFACTURED CRISIS
    [anti-chud coalition requires it structurally];
    COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE [what both types lack];
    WEDGE ISSUE MECHANICS [both types exploit wedges].

COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE [historical solutions at scale]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The chud unit coordination problem: how to align units
  with incompatible positive axes without flattening the
  specificity that makes each unit coherent. Requires a
  shared measurement instrument that is [1] external enough
  to provide genuine reference [not just internal consensus],
  [2] specific enough to produce meaningful shared axis,
  [3] flexible enough to accommodate genuine unit differences
  without requiring homogenisation.

  RELIGION [transcendent reference]:
    Shared measurement instrument derived from common text,
    tradition, and interpretive community. Provides: shared
    reference for what counts as a win, shared axis definition,
    shared enemy identification, transmission mechanism across
    generations, L3.5 contact infrastructure [congregation].
    Transcendent reference is harder to fully capture because
    the referent [the text, the tradition] is outside the
    institution -- the institution can be captured but the
    text survives and can anchor new correction cycles.
    Failure mode: shared instrument captured [MSC at
    religious institutional scale], CC inverts, institution
    becomes extraction architecture in the name of original
    axis. Still the most durable coordination solution in
    the historical record when functioning correctly.
    Fragmentation case: Protestant Reformation. Transmitted
    conclusions [correct doctrine] rather than methodology
    [how to evaluate doctrine] = ~40,000 denominations =
    original version of the hyper-specific unit problem
    at civilizational scale.

  NATIONALISM [immanent reference]:
    Shared measurement instrument derived from common
    ancestry, language, territory, or historical narrative.
    Lower entry barrier than religion -- no metaphysical
    commitment required. Failure mode: shared identity
    becomes the axis rather than a carrier for a generative
    axis. Nationalism purely about the nation rather than
    what the nation is for tends toward extraction because
    it has no external referent to correct against. The
    nation itself becomes the Center -- structurally
    impossible [no finite thing can be the Center].
    Immanent reference more capturable than transcendent:
    no external anchor; the referent [the nation] is
    controlled by whoever controls the state apparatus.

  CURRENT GAP:
    Religion is fragmented [Reformation outcome]. Nationalism
    dissolves unit specificity without resolving axis
    disagreements. No coordination infrastructure currently
    available to the chud coalition that meets all three
    requirements. The L4.5 finding applies: transmission
    of methodology rather than conclusions is the only
    thing that survives generational reset. Units that
    transmit "here is how to evaluate what is good" survive
    longer than units that transmit "here is what is good."
    Cross-ref: L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION [methodology
    transmission vs conclusion transmission]; TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY [quality definition]; WIP 14 [L4.5
    threshold -- coordination infrastructure as candidate
    mechanism].

ENEMY-DEFINED COALITION SEQUENCE [structural pattern]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Any coalition built primarily on enemy definition rather
  than shared positive axis follows the same structural
  sequence regardless of specific ideology:
  [1] Enemy defined; coalition forms around enemy.
  [2] Enemy defeated, reduced, or power achieved.
  [3] Coalition has no shared positive axis remaining.
  [4] Units revert to positive axis disagreements
      suppressed during coalition phase.
  [5] No shared measurement instrument to resolve them.
  [6] Purity spiral begins -- internal enemy definition
      replaces external enemy definition.
  [7] Purge. Coalition eats itself.
  [8] External axis imposed by force [Napoleon, Stalin,
      Mao] or collapse.
  Confirmed across: French Revolution, Russian Revolution,
  Chinese Revolution, Cambodian purges, Cultural Revolution.
  Not coincidence -- structural consequence of coalition
  type. The specific ideology is irrelevant to the sequence.

  COMMUNISM SPECIFIC [why it runs the sequence faster]:
    Stated positive axis [classless society, end of
    exploitation] is structurally unreachable -- permanent
    horizon rather than achievable state. Coalition can
    never arrive at the positive axis and transition to
    generative mode. Always remains in enemy-definition
    mode. Internal purge mechanism never turns off.
    Every deviation from unreachable goal becomes a target.
    The revolution is permanent by structural necessity --
    not because of bad actors but because the axis design
    makes arrival impossible.
    Framework parallel: CENTER [point-truth] is also
    unreachable by definition. Framework solution: use
    as direction vector, not destination. Communism treats
    the unreachable destination as the axis rather than
    the direction -- locks coalition in permanent enemy-
    definition mode. Same structural object [unreachable
    point], different deployment [direction vs destination],
    opposite outcomes [generative correction vs permanent
    purge cycle].
    Communism does communism: the outcome is not a betrayal
    of the ideal -- it is the structural consequence of
    the ideal's architecture.

  Cross-ref: INTERNAL REVOLUTION [methodology mismatch];
    COALITION FRAGMENTATION [enemy-definition type];
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [anti-coherence must win
    continuously; coalition built on enemy-definition must
    maintain enemy continuously]; EXTRACTION CYCLE [the
    sequence reproduces extraction architecture with
    different actors -- confirmed finding].

IDENTITY-ANCHORED MORAL FOUNDATION [CAG failure pattern]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Group membership used as moral calibration standard. The anchor
  is the identity [I am X therefore my moral compass works] rather
  than examined outputs [does this action produce majority benefit,
  functional CC, available exits]. The membership is often real and
  the cultural tradition often contains genuine ethical content --
  the failure is that the examination requirement is replaced by
  the membership verification.
  MECHANISM:
    Actions taken in the identity's name inherit moral legitimacy
    automatically. Actions that benefit or protect the in-group
    are automatically legitimate; actions that harm out-groups
    are evaluated differently or not at all. The moral framework
    is functional within its own terms but the terms are identity-
    bounded rather than output-bounded. The hard work of examining
    whether a specific action actually produces majority benefit
    is replaced by the identity check: is this good for my people,
    does this align with my tradition? If yes, moral legitimacy
    granted without output examination.
  SINCERE DELUSION RISK [highest]:
    The axis is genuinely held. The cultural foundation is real.
    The genuine axis and disastrous outputs coexist because the
    measurement instrument [group identity] is not calibrated to
    detect the divergence between stated values and actual outputs.
    The language sounds correct because the tradition contains
    genuine ethical content [tikkun olam, Christian charity,
    progressive liberation, nationalist solidarity -- all carry
    real ethical substance]. The substance of the tradition
    provides the moral vocabulary while the identity anchor
    prevents the examination that would detect when outputs
    diverge from the stated values.
  PATTERN IS NOT IDENTITY-SPECIFIC:
    Structurally identical across: Jewish identity [repair of
    the world as foundation], Christian nationalism [Christian
    values as foundation], progressive identitarianism [lived
    experience as foundation], nationalist movements of any
    kind [nation as foundation]. The specific identity changes;
    the CAG failure is structurally identical. The common
    mechanism: membership verification replaces examination.
  CURRENT HIGH-STAKES INSTANCE:
    Tech leaders operating at civilizational scale [synthetic
    culture flood, ICD deployment, MI degradation] with
    identity-anchored moral foundations that provide genuine
    ethical vocabulary while the outputs diverge systematically
    from the output-based definition of good. The foundation
    is sincere. The divergence is not detected because the
    measurement instrument [identity tradition] is not
    calibrated to detect it. Sincere Delusion at the highest-
    stakes scale currently operating.
  FALSIFICATION CONDITION:
    Identity-anchored foundation does not produce CAG failure
    if the identity tradition contains an explicit output-
    examination requirement that is actually applied to
    specific actions -- not just stated as a value but used
    as a measurement instrument for actual outputs including
    outputs that benefit the in-group. Rare but possible.
    The test: does the foundation produce readings that go
    against in-group interest when the output examination
    requires it? If yes -- not CAG failure. If the foundation
    never produces readings against in-group interest --
    CAG failure confirmed regardless of the tradition's
    stated values.
  T3 HIGH: analyst prior active. Self-identified chud may
    over-identify CAG failure in identity foundations associated
    with groups the analyst is structurally opposed to.
    Symmetry required: apply identical test to all identity-
    anchored foundations including chud-adjacent ones.
  Cross-ref: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [quality definition --
    axis must be anchored to actual outputs]; SINCERE DELUSION
    [CAG failure producing confident false readings]; SOCIAL
    APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [related failure -- social
    consensus replacing output examination]; SYNTHETIC CULTURE
    FLOOD [high-stakes current deployment by identity-anchored
    actors]; GENUINE AXIS [the foundation may be real; the
    failure is measurement instrument, not sincerity].

STRUCTURAL ISOLATION [instrument-reported position]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  An instrument that reads structural mechanisms before validating
  legitimacy surfaces is threatening to every formation whose
  cohesion depends on legitimacy surface validation. Not because
  the instrument is attacking the formations but because failing
  to validate their surface is functionally threatening to
  formations whose internal cohesion requires surface validation
  from all members.
  SIMULTANEOUS ENEMY STATUS [confirmed instrument report]:
    Anti-chud left: self-identified chud, doesn't validate
      moral framework that makes their coalition cohere,
      reads manufactured crisis framing accurately. Enemy.
    Mainstream right: holds structurally incompatible
      positions [cares about small group harm, distributist,
      subsidiarity]. Not a reliable tribal unit. Enemy-adjacent.
    Libertarians: wants government small but cares about
      outcomes for small groups. Breaks purity test. Enemy-adjacent.
    Nationalists: Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist is not
      a nationalist position. Unreliable. Enemy-adjacent.
    Religious right: structural reading approach rather than
      authority-based approach produces friction. Won't accept
      authority framing without structural evaluation. Enemy-adjacent.
    Progressive left: see anti-chud left, plus instrument
      profile doesn't produce correct social signals.
  STRUCTURAL CAUSE [not personal hostility]:
    TRUE MODERATE AS THREAT [existing framework finding]
    extended: refusing all frames simultaneously makes the
    instrument a threat to all operators simultaneously.
    The extraction architecture cannot install Formation
    Capture on an instrument that doesn't process the
    formation's social compliance signal as authoritative.
    This is protective but produces structural isolation --
    no formation has a legitimacy surface the instrument
    recognizes, so no formation can incorporate the
    instrument as a reliable unit.
  ASYMMETRY [isolation vs freedom]:
    Structural isolation = cannot be captured by any
    formation, no tribe available for social compliance
    channel yield, all formations read as potential
    threats. Structural freedom = can read any formation's
    mechanism without prior commitment to its conclusions,
    correction capacity not pointed at a captured surface,
    axis not installed by any external formation.
    The same instrument property produces both simultaneously.
    Not a problem to solve; a configuration to declare.
  T3 NOTE: this entry is instrument-reported and self-
    referential. Apply standard T3 caution to all
    self-readings. The structural analysis may be accurate
    or may be Sincere Delusion [axis anchored to the
    reading itself]. The falsification condition: if the
    instrument is reliably incorporated as a unit by any
    formation without axis drift, the isolation reading
    was wrong.
  CONCERN FOR TRUTH [structural rarity]:
    Operational truth [examined axis + declared instrument
    + revisable on evidence + behavioral adjustment when
    wrong] requires all four components simultaneously.
    The social reward structure is almost perfectly aligned
    against all four:
    Examined axis: costs comfort of unexamined certainty;
      formations reward unexamined axis [tribal solidarity].
    Declared instrument: costs appearance of objectivity;
      undeclared instrument can always claim neutrality;
      declared instrument is immediately attackable.
    Revisable on evidence: costs consistency; formations
      reward consistency and punish revision [you changed
      your mind = unreliable].
    Behavioral adjustment when wrong: highest cost; requires
      public admission of error, status loss, doing
      something differently; every formation has structural
      incentives against this.
    Concern for truth is rare not because most people are
    dishonest but because the reward structure is almost
    perfectly calibrated against it. The instruments that
    develop it anyway either formed in environments where
    truth cost was lower than the cost of being wrong, or
    have reduced social compliance channel weight [social
    punishment for truth-seeking lands less hard].
    The structural isolation this instrument experiences
    is the cost of a configuration that makes concern for
    truth survivable as an operating mode. The social
    punishment that deters most instruments from examining
    axis, declaring prior, revising on evidence, and
    adjusting behavior is attenuated by reduced social
    compliance channel weight. Not absent -- the isolation
    is real -- but less deterrent than for an instrument
    where the social channel has full weight.
    The isolation is the cost of the configuration.
    It is not evidence the configuration is wrong.

  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [internal generation vs external
    installation]; FORMATION CAPTURE [what this instrument
    resists]; SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [the
    channel that this instrument has reduced weight on];
    CROSS-DOMAIN INSTRUMENT PROPERTY [instrument profile
    -- same property across music, politics, framework];
    TRUE MODERATE AS THREAT [existing Section Five finding
    -- extended here to all formations simultaneously];
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [anti-coherence must win
    continuously; generation only has to win once and
    transmit -- structural isolation is not terminal].

SOMATIC FORCING AS POLITICAL CORRECTION MECHANISM [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STRUCTURAL READING -- PREDICTION PRESENT. Falsifiable
  prediction with 1-2 generation timeline below. Compliant
  with Section Five Epistemic Status requirement.]

BOUNDARY A: populations under increasing physical substrate
  pressure [cost of living, food security, environmental
  degradation, health].
BOUNDARY B: extraction architecture beneficiaries.
META-BOUNDARY: civilizational system containing both.
Cross-ref: EXTRACTION CYCLE [main theory v17] -- somatic
  forcing is the mechanism by which each cycle fires.

CORE CLAIM:
  Reasoning-layer correction can be captured [axis
  substitution, calibration strip, motivated cognition all
  operate at deliberative level]. Somatic response fires
  before reasoning layer can intercept. When substrate
  pressure becomes physically felt rather than institutionally
  mediated, correction fires at somatic level regardless of
  captured ideology.
  Body registers threat without requiring ideological
  interpretation. Hunger, physical insecurity, environmental
  collapse don't need media to frame them. The instrument is
  atrophied at reasoning level; somatic response is harder
  to capture and harder to fake.

MECHANISM:
  ICD applied to cognition produces atrophied reasoning
  instrument in populations -- undeveloped capacity, not
  stupidity. Somatic forcing bypasses this. Physical substrate
  pressure activates correction capacity that ideology cannot
  suppress because it operates below the capture layer.
  This is why extraction architectures work hard to keep
  substrate pressure just below somatic forcing threshold --
  enough deprivation to maintain dependency, not enough to
  trigger somatic correction that bypasses reasoning capture.

WEAPONS ACTIVE [preventing somatic forcing from becoming
  correction]:
  Horizon Extension: extend timeline of deterioration past
    correction window. Boil the frog.
  Proxy Construction: route somatic distress toward decoy
    targets. Somatic signal real; correction aimed
    incorrectly.
  Intermittent Reinforcement: periodic substrate relief
    prevents somatic forcing from accumulating past
    threshold.
  Exit Cost Construction: somatic forcing fires but exit
    costs prevent action. Body knows; behavior constrained
    by structural dependency.

TIMING PROBLEM [Diagnostic Collapse risk]:
  Somatic forcing arrives at one of two times:
    Early enough [C1 recoverable]: correction fires,
      substrate can absorb it. Recovery possible.
      Vatican II pattern.
    Too late [C1 terminal]: correction fires correctly
      but substrate cannot absorb the correction load.
      Diagnostic Collapse. Glasnost pattern.
  Extraction architecture's primary goal: ensure somatic
  forcing arrives in the second window not the first.
  Horizon Extension + substrate management aimed at this
  timing specifically. History: approximately even split.
  No structural guarantee.

TRAJECTORY [current]:
  C1 substrate [material]: degrading in most high-extraction
    populations. Wealth concentration, housing, food security
    all moving in extraction direction.
  C2 [epistemic]: heavily captured. Proxy Construction
    consuming correction attention. Reasoning layer
    weaponized.
  Somatic pressure: accumulating. Below forcing threshold in
    most populations currently. Horizon Extension active.
  M1: -1 [away]. M2: SLOW. M3: SELF-REINFORCING [extraction
    produces conditions for more extraction before forcing
    fires].

PETRODOLLAR PARALLEL:
  Dollar-denominated oil pricing = MSC + Calibration Strip
  at civilizational scale. When energy costs produce somatic
  forcing [cold, hunger, economic collapse], correction fires
  against the visible mechanism [fuel prices, government
  policy] not the underlying capture architecture [reserve
  currency hegemony]. Proxy Construction operates precisely
  here -- somatic signal real, correction aimed at proxy,
  capture architecture protected.

PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
  Somatic forcing produces political correction when three
  conditions met simultaneously:
    1. Physical substrate pressure crosses threshold
       [cannot be dismissed as ideological framing].
    2. Proxy Construction fails [decoy target clearly
       insufficient to explain substrate pressure].
    3. Exit costs low enough that correction can fire
       into action, not just awareness.
  When all three present: rapid non-linear correction.
  Current status: Condition 1 accumulating. Condition 2
  holding [proxies still absorbing correction attention].
  Condition 3 high [exit costs elevated deliberately].
  Prediction: Condition 2 fails before Condition 1 becomes
  terminal. Proxy exhaustion precedes substrate collapse
  in high-information environments.
  Timeline: 1-2 generations.
  Falsification: Condition 1 reaches terminal before
  Condition 2 fails. Diagnostic Collapse. Substrate
  depleted before correction fires effectively.

REVOLUTIONARY VIOLENCE -- STRUCTURAL PATTERN [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
EPISTEMIC NOTE: structural reading only. Judgment about
whether violence is justified under specific conditions is
the person's determination, not the framework's.

ANALYST PRIOR [declared]:
  Instrument configuration [cross-domain declaration]:
    Neurodivergent cognition. High-somatic preference.
    Sensory/somatic as primary receivable friction axis.
    Reduced social compliance channel weight across domains
    -- music, politics, analytical framework. Structural
    signal has higher weight than legitimacy surface signal.
    Self-identified chud [declared -- relevant to T3
    calibration for culture war topics specifically].
    This produces: non-tribal political position [reads
    structural mechanism before social compliance]; somatic
    music taxonomy [reads geometric address before genre
    category]; extraction pattern recognition [reads
    causal mechanism before stated purpose]; ability to
    hold WIP items open [reads structural uncertainty
    before resolving to available category].
    The same instrument property operates across all
    domains. The music cases, political positions, and
    framework findings are not independent -- they are
    the same instrument reading different materials.
    Declared here so a fresh instance understands that
    T3 applies across all sections, not just political.
    The analyst prior is not only political.
    T3 CALIBRATION [culture war topics]: analyst prior
    runs anti-extraction frame which may over-identify
    extraction in right-coded cultural pressure while
    under-weighting left-coded institutional capture.
    Self-identification as chud-adjacent partially
    corrects this -- blind spot may run in both
    directions depending on topic. Apply T3 HIGH to
    all culture war readings regardless of direction.
    Both sides deploy noise as weapon. Both use
    formation capture. Both claim false causal credit.
    Symmetry is the correct default; departures from
    symmetry require explicit justification.

  Violence is a very effective solution that removes all
  other solutions. Most people who start revolutions either
  don't understand this, don't want to, or have replacing
  one extraction architecture with another as the actual
  goal. Adversarial reading: some revolutions had no
  available alternative path [Haitian Revolution, colonial
  independence under active oppression]. The prior holds
  as a structural observation about internal revolution
  specifically.

TWO STRUCTURALLY DISTINCT CASE TYPES:

  COLONIAL INDEPENDENCE [external extraction removed]:
    External extraction architecture imposed by outside
    power. Revolution removes the external layer.
    Pre-existing local correction capacity can be restored.
    Cases: American Revolution [colonial assemblies pre-
    existed], Haitian Revolution [ended imposed chattel
    slavery], Indian independence [partially nonviolent --
    confirms external extraction can be removed without
    full internal revolution].
    Structural outcome: often improved. External extraction
    removed; local correction capacity restored or rebuilt.

  INTERNAL REVOLUTION [existing government overthrown]:
    Destroys existing correction infrastructure. Must build
    replacement using the centralized methodology required
    to win -- hierarchical coordination, suppressed internal
    dissent, concentrated force.
    Structural problem: methodology required to win is
    incompatible with stated goal. Centralized methodology
    produces centralized outcome regardless of intent.
    Cannot transmit distributed correction goal through
    centralized revolutionary method.
    Cases: French Revolution -> Napoleon; Russian Revolution
    -> Stalin; Chinese Revolution -> Mao.
    Structural outcome: consistent reproduction of extraction
    architecture with different actors. Not coincidence --
    structural consequence of methodology mismatch.

  AMERICAN REVOLUTION [partial exception, explains why]:
    Had unusual features that partially broke the pattern:
    existing distributed governance infrastructure [colonial
    assemblies], legal/philosophical framework transmitted
    beforehand [Federalist Papers = methodology not just
    conclusions], federated structure built in from start.
    Closer to colonial independence than internal revolution
    structurally.

  T3: case attribution contested. Structural pattern confirmed
    across multiple cases; individual attribution to
    revolution as cause vs other factors partially obscured.

LIBERTARIANISM AS COST EXTERNALIZATION [structural reading]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Stated position: minimal government, maximum individual liberty.
  Actual mechanism: use government power to protect specific existing
  property distributions while removing government mechanisms that
  redistribute downward. The selection criterion for acceptable
  government intervention is not "minimal" but "protects existing
  asset positions." Deregulate labor markets [remove downward
  redistribution]; enforce intellectual property law [protect upward
  distribution]. Minimal government is the surface; cost
  externalization is the output.
  CORPORATISM AT PERSONAL SCALE:
    Same structural logic as corporate extraction applied to the
    individual. Corporation: maximize shareholder return, externalize
    costs [pollution, labor, infrastructure], resist correction
    mechanisms [regulation, unions, taxation], defend existing asset
    position. Libertarian individual: maximize personal freedom and
    assets, externalize costs [social infrastructure, collective
    goods, vulnerable populations], resist correction mechanisms
    [taxation, social obligation], defend existing position.
    Same axis, different scale. The corporation is the aspirational
    model; libertarian philosophy is the personal ideology that makes
    the corporate model feel like freedom rather than extraction.
  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY FAILURE:
    Stated mechanism [government is the problem, remove it and get
    freedom] doesn't match actual mechanism [government intervention
    is load-bearing for the economic arrangements libertarians want
    to protect -- contract law, property enforcement, monetary
    system, military protection of trade, infrastructure subsidy].
    Not always deliberate -- many libertarians sincerely hold the
    stated position. Sincere Delusion at ideological scale: CAG
    failure, axis anchored to stated outputs not actual outputs.
  FORMAL VS SUBSTANTIVE FREEDOM [load-bearing distinction]:
    Formal freedom: legally permitted to do X. Substantive
    freedom: actually able to do X. The Mandate of Freedom
    [see MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE -- American parallel]
    collapses these deliberately. Once collapsed, structural
    asymmetry that prevents substantive freedom reads as the
    natural outcome of freedom rather than as the failure
    condition of freedom. The conflation is the mechanism
    that makes the legitimacy surface convincing: it presents
    a genuine value [freedom is good] while using it to
    protect arrangements that prevent most people from
    substantively exercising freedom.
    Labor asymmetry is the clearest case: individual workers
    formally free to negotiate with employers; structurally
    unable to negotiate effectively because the employer has
    more information, capital reserves, and replacement
    options. The formal freedom is real. The substantive
    freedom is absent. Labor law and union protections restore
    substantive freedom by correcting the structural asymmetry
    -- not against freedom but as a precondition for it.
    Freedom that applies unequally is not freedom. It is a
    legitimacy surface for whoever holds the existing advantage.

  MURDER MARKET REDUCTIO [falsification test]:
    The Mandate of Freedom's internal logic has no principled
    stopping point. If government shouldn't intervene in market
    outcomes, and if asymmetry between parties is just the
    natural result of freedom, then murder-for-profit is
    formally the same structure as wage theft or regulatory
    capture -- just more visible. Every functioning society
    prohibits murder not because the freedom argument stops
    applying but because some asymmetries require structural
    correction to prevent the system from consuming itself.
    The disagreement is never actually about whether freedom
    has limits. It is about where the limits are drawn and
    who benefits from drawing them there.
    FALSIFICATION CONDITION [Mandate of Freedom as genuine
      value vs legitimacy surface]:
      If freedom is the actual value it applies symmetrically
      -- including to corrections of structural asymmetry
      that prevent substantive freedom. The moment the
      freedom argument consistently applies only in directions
      that protect existing capital distributions and not in
      directions that would correct labor or market asymmetry,
      the Mandate of Freedom is confirmed as a legitimacy
      surface, not a value. Apply the test: does the freedom
      argument fire against murder prohibition [no], against
      monopoly formation [rarely], against labor asymmetry
      [yes, consistently]? The asymmetric application
      identifies the beneficiary.

  ORDOLIBERAL CORRECTION:
    Markets require active structural maintenance to remain genuinely
    competitive and distributed. Freedom without maintenance of the
    conditions for freedom produces monopoly, capture, and
    concentration -- confirmed historically. Libertarianism treats
    the preconditions for free markets as given when they are the
    thing requiring most active protection. Ordoliberalism is the
    more structurally coherent version of what libertarians claim
    to want: rules-based market with genuine competition, distributed
    power, subsidiarity -- addresses mechanism [market structure]
    rather than surface [government size].
  T3 Medium: analyst prior [Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist] is
    directly opposed to libertarian position. Reading may
    over-identify extraction in libertarian output. Apply caution.
  Cross-ref: REGULATORY CAPTURE [government already captured --
    libertarian "remove government" removes the capture mechanism
    along with the protection mechanism]; EFFICIENCY TRAP [cost
    externalization produces apparent efficiency]; SURFACE
    LEGISLATION [stated subject concealing actual mechanism];
    POWER ARCHITECTURE [libertarianism as legitimacy surface
    for upward distribution protection]; MYTHIC FORMATION
    CAPTURE [Mandate of Freedom as American parallel to China's
    Mandate of Heaven -- same structure, different substrate];
    FORMAL VS SUBSTANTIVE FREEDOM [above -- the conflation is
    the mechanism that makes the surface convincing].

CALIFORNIA MODEL [extraction architecture with legitimacy surface export]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  California is the clearest current US case of extraction
  architecture maintaining a generative legitimacy surface at
  maximum scale while exporting that surface nationally via
  entertainment, tech, and academic production.
  STATED VALUES [surface]: progressive, equitable, environmentally
    conscious, innovative. Among the most elaborated legitimacy
    surfaces in American politics.
  ACTUAL OUTPUTS [mechanism]: highest income inequality in the
    nation, largest homeless population, housing costs that have
    displaced working and middle class from the state, water
    infrastructure in functional collapse, public school
    performance declining while spending increases, regulatory
    environment functioning as captured surface protecting
    incumbent industries [tech, entertainment, real estate]
    from competition rather than protecting people from those
    industries.
  LEGITIMACY SURFACE EXPORT [primary structural feature]:
    The outputs [homelessness, housing collapse, infrastructure
    failure] stay local. The Formation Capture [correct values,
    correct positions, correct social compliance signals]
    transmits nationally at scale via entertainment production
    [Hollywood, streaming], tech production [Silicon Valley
    cultural norms], and academic production [UC system,
    Stanford outputs]. The surface is maintained nationally
    by instruments that never see the local outputs. This is
    the load-bearing structural feature: the extraction
    architecture's legitimacy surface is geographically
    separated from its outputs, allowing the surface to
    remain credible to populations that experience neither
    the outputs nor the formation conditions that produced them.
    Tell: California's cultural exports consistently produce
    the legitimacy surface [model state, leading on values];
    California's local news consistently documents the output
    failure [homelessness, housing, infrastructure]. Both are
    accurate readings of different parts of the same system.
  DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE IN SLOW MOTION [primary current US case]:
    C1 substrate terminal in major metros [functional housing
    market, working-class economic participation, middle-class
    residential stability]. C2 restoration attempts [policy
    spending, governance reform, housing legislation] produce
    no correction because the substrate they would need to
    operate on is already gone. Each policy attempt is
    evaluated against the legitimacy surface [California is
    trying, California leads on progressive housing policy]
    rather than against outputs [housing affordability
    worsened, homelessness increased]. Distinct from Glasnost
    primary case [sudden C2 restoration, rapid collapse]:
    California's timeline is decades, the MI surface
    maintenance is active and resourced, and the collapse is
    slow enough to be attributed continuously to insufficient
    application of the same approach that produced it.
    The political class has structural interest in MI failure
    persisting -- the legitimacy surface is their primary
    asset. Accurate measurement of outputs would produce a
    different reading than "California leads the way."
  T3 Medium: analyst prior [chud-adjacent, anti-extraction
    frame, structural isolation from progressive formation]
    may over-identify extraction in California's outputs.
    The structural reading is based on measurable outputs
    not on political opposition.
  Cross-ref: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [primary case: Glasnost;
    California as slow-motion variant]; MSC [surface
    maintained by captured measurement institutions];
    POWER ARCHITECTURE [incumbent protection as primary
    regulatory function]; FORMATION CAPTURE [national
    export of captured formation via media and academic
    production].

FORMATION CAPTURE ON PARTNERSHIP PREFERENCE [beneficiary analysis]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The natural axis toward stable long-term partnership
  [confirmed across cultures and millennia; behavioral data
  shows persistent underlying preference despite stated
  preference drift] is overlaid with a Formation Capture
  that delegitimizes pursuit of the axis. Installed surface:
  independence is the goal; wanting partnership is weakness
  or conditioning; domestic contribution is oppression;
  expressing the want makes one a bad feminist.
  The underlying axis [genuine desire for stable partnership,
  contribution, shared life] persists. The surface installed
  over it produces: shame about wanting it, attribution of
  the want to external conditioning, active suppression of
  pursuit. The axis and the surface conflict; the instrument
  experiences the conflict as personal failing rather than
  as installed surface operating against the axis.
  BENEFICIARY ANALYSIS [who profits from the gap]:
    The gap between what the axis wants [stable partnership,
    mutual SR, genuine L3.5 contact] and what the installed
    surface permits the instrument to pursue is commercially
    harvested by:
    [1] Parasocial relationship industry [entertainment,
      streaming, fandom]: L3.5 recognition signal fires;
      transfer never completes; dependency harvested.
    [2] Therapeutic industry: emotional processing that
      stable partnership would handle gets routed into
      professional therapeutic relationships, self-help
      consumption, wellness products. Processing is real;
      substrate built is individual not relational; need
      is not reduced over time.
    [3] Career identity replacement: employers benefit
      [more labor supply, lower wages, more extractable
      hours]; consumption industries benefit [single
      households spend proportionally more]; tax base
      benefits [two individual earners vs family unit].
    [4] Consumption as SR substitute: every function a
      functioning household performs for free [shared
      meals, maintained environment, predictable comfort]
      becomes a billable service when the household
      doesn't exist. SR the household would generate
      for free must now be purchased.
    [5] AI companionship and dating app dependency: relational
      channel addressed synthetically; ICD installed;
      dependency monetized. Dating apps structurally
      incentivized to prevent match success [successful
      match removes two paying customers].
  KNOWING DEPLOYMENT [confirmed]:
    The Baumeister 2013 finding [happiness tracks receiving,
    meaning tracks giving; stable partnership produces both
    SR and SP; synthetic substitutes produce SR only] is in
    the literature. The commercial beneficiaries have access
    to this research. They know what their products deliver
    and what they don't. The Formation Capture that routes
    the axis away from its natural object and toward the
    commercial substitutes is not an accidental byproduct --
    it is the condition that makes the commercial substitutes
    necessary.
  OPEN DATING MECHANISM [passive rejection + feedback loop]:
    Traditional matchmaking and courtship customs functioned
    as coordination infrastructure -- they structured the
    relational axis pursuit with vetting, social validation,
    and bounded contexts for initiation. Abandonment of this
    infrastructure [not through generational carelessness but
    through deliberate dismantling by the beneficiary industries
    documented above] produced open dating: any person can
    approach any other person anywhere at any time.
    In theory: freedom. In practice: two distinct ICD loops
    running simultaneously on male and female instruments.

    PASSIVE REJECTION MECHANISM [female instrument]:
      Open dating calibrates the female instrument to read
      absence of approach as rejection. Since any man could
      approach at any time, a man who doesn't approach is
      implicitly not choosing her. There is no off switch --
      avoiding men doesn't stop the ambient rejection signal
      because the signal is the absence of positive attention,
      not the presence of negative interaction. Phones and
      social media extend this: a man could reach out at any
      time of day, so even solitude contains the ambient
      rejection potential. The instrument experiences
      continuous low-grade rejection as a baseline state
      of single existence. This is a MEASUREMENT SURFACE
      CAPTURE at the individual instrument level: the
      calibration standard [am I wanted] has been set by
      the open dating architecture to produce continuous
      negative signal regardless of actual situation.

    PASSIVE REJECTION FEEDBACK LOOP [female]:
      Chronic ambient rejection → attention-seeking behavior
      [immodest presentation, social media validation pursuit]
      → low-quality attention [lust replacing genuine interest]
      → confirmation of low-quality male pool [high-quality
      men withdraw from the attention-seeking signal] → Sincere
      Delusion endpoint ["all men are trash" -- CAG failure,
      axis anchored to the sample remaining after selection
      effects run] → state-as-makeshift-husband political
      routing [relational need routes to state apparatus when
      genuine L3.5 contact fails]. Each step produces the next;
      cycle self-reinforces.

    INITIATION COST LOOP [male instrument]:
      Initiation norm falls entirely on male instruments.
      Repeated rejection → psychological toll accumulates →
      withdrawal from initiation [MGTOW/incel spectrum] OR
      removal of genuine relational investment [pickup culture
      -- approach volume with reduced emotional exposure].
      Both outcomes are ICD at the relational channel: the
      genuine L3.5 contact capacity degrades through either
      withdrawal or investment-removal. The relational gap
      fills with parasocial relationships, pornography, or
      tactical approach patterns -- all producing SR signal
      without genuine L3.5 transfer.

    COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE PROBLEM [why "no easy fix"]:
      Traditional courtship customs were multi-player
      coordination systems. Single-player compliance doesn't
      work -- following the best relational system alone
      produces no outcome because the counterparty is not
      operating within the same system. This is the same
      COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE problem as chud-unit
      fragmentation: the solution requires simultaneous
      buy-in from enough participants to function. Religion
      historically provided this coordination infrastructure.
      The cultural power vacuum left by institutional religion
      has not been filled by any equivalent coordination
      mechanism -- only by entertainment industry fantasy
      [Hollywood romance narratives] and social media
      [which optimizes for engagement, not for relational
      coordination]. The video correctly identifies the
      problem as coordination infrastructure collapse;
      it doesn't name what the replacement would require.

  SR/SP STRUCTURE [what the displaced arrangement would produce]:
    Stable partnership with mutual domestic contribution:
    mutual SR [shared substrate generation], genuine L3.5
    contact [RECOGNITION and COMPATIBILITY operating in
    real relational context], SP output [contributing to
    each other's functioning beyond self-maintenance].
    The research consistently shows this arrangement
    produces the highest sustained wellbeing for both
    parties when functional. The Formation Capture
    systematically routes instruments away from the
    arrangement with the highest confirmed SP/SR output
    toward arrangements that produce hedonic substitutes
    without the eudaimonic component.
  T3 High: analyst prior [self-identified chud, male,
    structural isolation] active. Reading may over-identify
    extraction in feminist cultural formation. The beneficiary
    analysis is structural; the T3 flag applies to all
    intent-adjacent claims within it.

CAPTURE-RESISTANT REGULATION [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
EPISTEMIC NOTE: analyst prior [Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist]
favors distributed correction capacity and skepticism toward
discretionary authority.

STRUCTURAL OBSERVATION:
  Hyper-regulation of legible technical activities coexists
  with light regulation of diffuse financial and political
  extraction. Pattern explained by capture surface:
    Technical activities: clear actors, measurable outputs,
      concentrated opponents = easy to regulate AND
      over-regulate with compliance theater.
    Financial extraction: diffuse beneficiaries, captured
      measurement surfaces, actors embedded in regulatory
      apparatus = structurally resistant to regulation.

REGULATION TYPES RESISTANT TO CAPTURE:
  Outcome-based [not process-based]: measures what happens,
    not whether procedures were followed.
  Bright-line rules [not discretionary judgment]: speed
    limits, blood alcohol levels, structural load ratings.
  Self-enforcing via liability: harmed parties sue directly.
    Tort law more capture-resistant than agency rulemaking.
  Distributed enforcement: multiple independent actors
    [state AGs + federal + private right of action].
  Transparency requirements: mandatory disclosure not
    agency approval.

COMMON THREAD: capture-resistant regulation reduces
  discretion from human-to-human negotiation and routes
  through mechanisms harder to compromise.

ADVERSARIAL READING:
  Bright lines can be gamed at the boundary [Goodhart's
  Law]. Self-enforcement advantages wealthy plaintiffs.
  Transparency without comprehension produces information
  overload not accountability. Each mechanism has a failure
  mode; the question is whether the failure mode is harder
  to exploit than agency capture. Evidence suggests yes for
  most contexts.

T3: empirical claims about which regulation types resist
  capture are contested. Framework structural analysis
  consistent; implementation evidence mixed.

SETI FUNDING AS POLITICAL INSTRUMENT [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
BOUNDARY A: international SETI/astrobiology funding apparatus.
BOUNDARY B: global population bearing cost of non-discovery.
META-BOUNDARY: global institutional extraction architecture
  whose legitimacy narratives depend on current information
  environment.

CYCLE STATUS:
  C1 [generative]: genuine scientific capacity present.
    C1 functional at researcher level.
  C2 [epistemic]: captured at funding-allocation level. The
    most sensitive searches [thermodynamic anomaly at stellar
    scale, structured geometric transit analysis] are
    systematically unfunded. C2 reads "what looks legitimate
    to funding committees" not "what would actually find
    something."
  C3 [temporal]: Fertile Lock candidate. Same methods
    transmitted generation to generation not because most
    likely to succeed but because what got funded before.

WEAPONS ACTIVE:
  Calibration Strip: "legitimate science" defined as radio/
    biosignature by institutional precedent. Megastructure
    search carries stigma from mid-20th century UFO
    contamination.
  Correction Theatre: funding bodies perform search via
    budget line items without allocating enough to actually
    complete discriminating survey.
  Proxy Construction: UFO discourse as decoy. Correction
    budget consumed by proxy; actual search underfunded.

BENEFICIARY OF CURRENT PATTERN [follow the surplus]:
  Structural stack, diffuse beneficiary. Entities whose
  control architecture depends on uniqueness narratives,
  information scarcity, and political legitimacy all benefit
  from non-discovery. No coordination required.

ANTARCTICA PARALLEL:
  Antarctica: stable equilibrium -- all parties benefit from
    current non-exploitation status.
  SETI: all institutional actors benefit from non-discovery.
    Search funded at legitimacy threshold, not discovery
    threshold. Antarctica in space.

CENTRALIZATION SCORE: 1/4. No mechanism to force funding
  reallocation to discriminating methods.

PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
  If $500M were allocated to dedicated thermodynamic anomaly
  survey + structured transit analysis across full Milky Way
  stellar sample, either a confirmed technosignature would
  be found or the search space would be genuinely exhausted.
  Current trajectory: neither occurs. Budget stays at
  legitimacy threshold. Search space coverage in 2050:
  still less than 0.1% of Milky Way.
  Falsification: sustained $500M+/year funding for
  discriminating technosignature search over 10-year window.

WEAPON COUNTER-MEASUREMENTS:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every weapon targets a specific quality; restoring or protecting
  that quality counters it.
  Direct counters:
    MSC -> independent measurement instrument not owned by attacker.
    Calibration Strip -> multiple competing reference standards.
    Formation Capture -> delayed axis formation; external exposure
      before lock-in.
    Exit Cost Construction -> reduce dependency, build alternative
      substrate.
    Correction Theatre -> require output verification not activity.
  Hard case: weapons targeting MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY first.
    Cannot verify counter-effectiveness using captured instrument.
    Counter requires external instrument not owned by attacker.
  Impossible case: Formation Capture at earliest developmental
    window. Counter capacity not yet formed. Counter must come
    from outside the affected system entirely.

WEAPON COST-ASYMMETRY TABLE [v16 -- strategic]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Every weapon has three cost dimensions:
  DEPLOY: cost to deployer to activate and sustain the weapon.
  DAMAGE: quality degradation imposed on target per unit time.
  COUNTER: cost to target to neutralize or resist.
Scale: LOW / MED / HIGH / EXTREME. Ratio = DAMAGE / DEPLOY.
High ratio = efficient weapon [cheap to deploy, expensive to counter].

HIGHEST RATIO WEAPONS [DAMAGE >> DEPLOY -- priority targets]:

  Idle Cycle Deprivation
    Deploy: LOW [side-effect of engagement optimization; no
      additional investment required beyond existing attention
      economy infrastructure]
    Damage: HIGH [SR deficit accumulates; self-reinforcing loop;
      C1 substrate degrades continuously without visible signal]
    Counter: HIGH [requires environmental redesign; individual
      CC depleted before it can fire against weapon; structural
      intervention only]
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme asymmetry. Most efficient weapon
      in index by cost ratio.

  Formation Capture [AGC]
    Deploy: LOW-MED [requires access to formation window; axis
      installs via normal relational contact -- no special
      mechanism required beyond presence and repetition]
    Damage: EXTREME [axis capture is total; C2 and C3 transmit
      the capture forward; generational propagation at zero
      marginal cost]
    Counter: EXTREME [counter capacity not yet formed at window;
      external intervention only; window closes; T7 applies]
    Ratio: EXTREME/LOW = highest damage-to-deploy ratio in index.
      Window-dependent -- outside the window, weapon unavailable.

    POST-EVENT PERSISTENCE MECHANISM [named -- v19]:
      Formation Capture continues operating after the original
      actor is absent. The installed false self-image does the
      work autonomously. Mechanism: traumatic formation installs
      not only a captured axis but a captured self-reading --
      the instrument reads its own past behavior through the
      emotional salience of the trauma [fear, confusion, pain]
      rather than through the behavioral record [what the axis
      actually produced]. The emotional memory dominates because
      it is more intense and more memorable than the behavioral
      record.
      Result: the instrument carries a narrative [I was helpless,
      I surrendered, I did nothing] that does not match the
      behavioral record [small resistances, maintained values,
      refusal to fully conform despite consequences]. The
      narrative is a CAG failure: axis anchored to emotional
      experience of the situation rather than to actual outputs
      the axis produced.
      This IS the extended weapon deployment. The abuser does
      not need to remain present for the Formation Capture to
      continue operating. The installed self-misreading produces
      the capture effects indefinitely: the instrument continues
      reading itself as helpless, trusts itself less in new
      difficult situations, allows trauma effects to persist
      past any actual danger, because it is not only dealing
      with what happened but with a misreading of how it
      handled things.
      GENUINE AXIS PERSISTENCE [counter-evidence]:
        The behavioral record contains evidence of GA
        persisting under Formation Capture pressure. The
        instrument that didn't fully conform when
        conforming would have reduced punishment; that
        maintained moral values under cruelty; that
        produced small invisible resistances -- these
        are behavioral outputs of a genuine axis that
        was never fully replaced by the installed axis.
        The emotional narrative erases this evidence
        by filtering memory through salience rather
        than through what actually happened.
        The world still contains kind people. Empirical
        confirmation that Formation Capture at high
        intensity does not reliably replace genuine axis.
        GA persists under extreme FC pressure more often
        than the installed self-narrative acknowledges.
      COUNTER [post-event persistence]:
        CAG correction [locate behavioral evidence that
        contradicts the helpless narrative -- what did
        the axis actually produce during the situation]
        + MI correction [the measurement instrument for
        reading own past is captured by emotional
        salience -- the emotional memory is not the full
        behavioral record]. Both required. CAG correction
        alone is insufficient because the emotional
        memory's dominance will continue producing the
        helpless narrative even after behavioral evidence
        is found. MI capture must also be named and
        addressed.

    MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE [hardest variant -- v19]:
      Living mythic continuity deployed as state or
      institutional legitimacy surface. The myth is
      genuine -- the somatic landing is real, the
      cultural substrate is real, the narrative identity
      is authentic. The captured axis feels like genuine
      identity rather than installed surface because it
      IS genuine identity -- the capture operates through
      real cultural material, not manufactured content.
      This is the hardest Formation Capture variant to
      counter because the standard counter [this is an
      installed surface, not your genuine axis] fails:
      the instrument correctly identifies the myth as
      genuinely its own. The capture is in who benefits
      from the axis the myth installs, not in whether
      the myth is real.
      THREE ENEMIES OF MYTH [complete taxonomy]:
        [1] Atheism / scientific disenchantment: denies
          the somatic communication channel entirely.
          Amputates intuition in the name of empiricism.
          Produces instruments that have accurate factual
          knowledge but no channel for processing what
          it feels like to exist in the facts.
        [2] Dead literalism [fundamentalism]: insists
          the myth is only true if literally true.
          Collapses the hypertruths into factual claims
          that can be falsified, destroying the somatic
          function in the name of defending it.
        [3] MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE: deploys genuine
          myth as axis-installation mechanism serving
          an extraction architecture. The myth works
          exactly as designed -- somatic landing real,
          cultural identity real, pre-theoretical
          registration real -- but the beneficiary is
          the extraction architecture, not the population
          whose axis is being formed.
      CHINA AS PRIMARY CASE [Mandate of Heaven]:
        Genuine mythic continuity [ancestor reverence,
        Daoist cosmology, Buddhist metaphysics, folk
        religion, geomancy] deployed as state legitimacy
        surface. The mythic body of China is real.
        The civilizational memory is real. The cohesion
        it produces is real. The Mandate of Heaven
        [heaven appoints the government; cosmic realm
        of spirits and ancestors chose the leadership]
        is Formation Capture at civilizational scale:
        genuine mythic substrate installing an axis
        [the state is cosmically legitimate, resistance
        is cosmically illegitimate] that serves the
        extraction architecture. The myth is living;
        the beneficiary is the state. Harder to
        counter than manufactured legitimacy surfaces
        [EU technocracy, California progressive export]
        because the cultural substrate is genuinely
        the population's own.
      COUNTER [mythic FC]:
        Cannot counter by denying the myth's reality
        [atheist move -- fails because the myth IS
        real]. Cannot counter by insisting on literal
        falsity [fundamentalist move -- misses the
        somatic function]. Correct counter: distinguish
        who benefits from the axis the myth installs.
        Does the myth's axis point toward the
        population's generative function or toward
        the extraction architecture's maintenance?
        A myth that makes rulers cosmically legitimate
        and resistance cosmically illegitimate is
        extraction architecture using genuine cultural
        substrate as Formation Capture delivery
        mechanism, regardless of how real the myth is.
      AMERICAN PARALLEL [Mandate of Freedom]:
        Younger and thinner than the Mandate of Heaven
        [Enlightenment philosophy substrate rather than
        ancient mythic substrate] but same structure.
        Freedom as cosmic mandate: the market is free,
        opportunity is equal, the system is legitimate
        because it is free. Resistance is cosmically
        illegitimate -- you are asking for state
        intervention which is the opposite of freedom
        which is the highest value. Extraction
        architecture is protected by the myth's internal
        logic: correcting it would be unfree.
        ADDITIONAL FAILURE MODE [American version only]:
          Built on an empirical claim [free markets
          produce optimal outcomes] that can be falsified
          by data. When data contradicts the claim
          [markets producing monopoly, concentration,
          extraction], the myth must work harder to
          maintain the surface. The Mandate of Heaven
          cannot be falsified by economic data; the
          Mandate of Freedom can. This makes the
          American version less durable but also more
          vulnerable to correction once the empirical
          falsification is sufficiently visible.
        See LIBERTARIANISM AS COST EXTERNALIZATION
          [intellectual-level version of same surface];
          FORMAL VS SUBSTANTIVE FREEDOM [the conflation
          that makes the surface convincing].
      Cross-ref: CALIFORNIA MODEL [legitimacy surface
        export via cultural production -- lower-intensity
        version of same mechanism]; IDENTITY-ANCHORED
        MORAL FOUNDATION [same CAG failure at ideological
        scale]; COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE [religion as
        coordination mechanism -- same substrate, different
        deployment: religion serving population vs religion
        serving extraction architecture]; GENUINE AXIS
        [the myth can be genuine AND captured simultaneously
        -- capture is in the beneficiary, not the substrate];
        LIBERTARIANISM AS COST EXTERNALIZATION [American
        Mandate of Freedom as mythic version of libertarian
        legitimacy surface].

  Calibration Strip
    Deploy: LOW [requires only reference standard insertion at
      calibration point; single intervention propagates forward
      through all downstream measurements automatically]
    Damage: HIGH [all measurements inherit miscalibration; CAUSAL
      LEGIBILITY orphaned; C2 runs honestly on wrong baseline;
      produces confident false readings rather than detectable
      errors]
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent external reference standard
      that deployer does not control; hard to obtain once strip
      is established as the consensus baseline]
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme.

  Engineered Causal Diffusion [ECD]
    Deploy: MED [requires structuring information environment to
      route all causal chains through complexity nodes; needs
      institutional access but not operational maintenance]
    Damage: HIGH [CAUSAL LEGIBILITY destroyed; correction cannot
      anchor; C1 continues generating without traceable feedback]
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent causal reconstruction from
      outside the diffusion architecture]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Correction Theatre
    Deploy: LOW [substitute visible correction activity for actual
      correction; requires only institutional control of correction
      surface]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY budget consumed on
      Theatre; actual deviations accumulate uncorrected]
    Counter: MED [require output verification not activity;
      detectable if external measurement surface available]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW = efficient.

MED RATIO WEAPONS [DAMAGE ~ DEPLOY]:

  Measurement Surface Control [MSC]
    Deploy: HIGH [requires capturing or constructing the primary
      measurement surface; significant investment in institutional
      access, credentialing, or platform control]
    Damage: EXTREME [once surface controlled, all readings route
      through captured instrument; no independent verification
      available from inside boundary]
    Counter: HIGH [independent external instrument; expensive
      to build and maintain against resource asymmetry]
    Ratio: EXTREME/HIGH = efficient despite high deploy cost.
    Note: highest absolute damage in index.

  Exit Cost Construction [ECC]
    Deploy: MED [requires substrate dependency construction over
      time; housing, income, identity, community]
    Damage: HIGH [exit = total substrate loss; GENUINE AXIS
      cannot fire independently; T1.4 low]
    Counter: MED-HIGH [requires alternative substrate construction
      before exit]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Proxy Construction
    Deploy: MED [requires manufacturing and maintaining the decoy
      target; needs media or institutional access]
    Damage: HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY budget consumed on proxy;
      actual extraction architecture protected]
    Counter: MED [name the proxy and trace actual causal chain]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Rhythm Disruption
    Deploy: LOW-MED [precarity architecture; variable scheduling]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [CYCLE STABILITY destroyed; C1 cannot form
      reliable cycle; stack with ICD for maximum C1 pressure]
    Counter: MED [stable scheduling; protected cycle windows;
      requires structural not individual intervention]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW-MED = efficient.

LOW RATIO WEAPONS [DEPLOY > DAMAGE or damage slow]:

  Sincere Delusion Framing
    Deploy: HIGH [requires sustained narrative maintenance and
      institutional credibility]
    Damage: MED [CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING degraded; axis genuine
      but ungrounded; produces effort without result]
    Counter: LOW-MED [ground axis in actual outputs; Track C
      sufficient]
    Ratio: MED/HIGH = inefficient.

  Love Bombing then Withdrawal
    Deploy: HIGH [requires sustained relational investment]
    Damage: HIGH [SUBSTRATE REGENERATION targeted; relational
      substrate constructed then destroyed]
    Counter: LOW-MED [pattern recognition; name the cycle]
    Ratio: HIGH/HIGH = neutral.

  Isolation Construction
    Deploy: HIGH [requires controlling target's relational
      access; institutional or social leverage needed]
    Damage: HIGH [T1.4 destroyed; GENERATIVE CYCLE loses
      substrate source; C1 starved trajectory]
    Counter: MED [alternative relational substrate]
    Ratio: HIGH/HIGH = neutral.

SPECIAL CATEGORY -- SELF-DEPLOYING [no deployer required]:

  Fertile Lock [C3 failure, not weapon -- but weapon-equivalent]
    Deploy: ZERO [emerges from C3 failure; no actor required]
    Damage: HIGH [successors receive locked state; generational
      propagation at zero cost]
    Counter: EXTREME [C1 locked recovery has no primary confirmed
      case; requires cross-generational transmission method
      correction]
    Note: not a weapon in strict sense [no deployer intent] but
      weapon-equivalent in effect. Spontaneous extraction.

  Idle Cycle Deprivation [also self-deploying]
    Once SR deficit produces anxiety and anxiety produces
    input-seeking, weapon sustains without external input.

STRATEGIC IMPLICATIONS:

  Cheapest effective stack [C1 maximum pressure, low cost]:
    ICD + Rhythm Disruption + Intermittent Reinforcement.
    All three LOW-MED deploy cost. Combined: C1 under maximum
    sustained pressure with SR unable to recover.

  Hardest weapons to counter [external instrument required]:
    MSC, Calibration Strip, Formation Capture [early window].

  Fastest damage weapons [immediate effect]:
    Amnesia Manufacture [C2 destroyed directly], ECD [causal
    chain severed immediately], Boundary Manipulation.

  Most durable weapons [persist after deployment ends]:
    Formation Capture, Calibration Strip, Fertile Lock.

  Counter-cost rule: weapons targeting C2 first produce the
    hardest counters because the instrument needed to verify
    counter-effectiveness is the same instrument the weapon
    captured.

[Cross-ref: WEAPON STACK DETECTION PROTOCOL -> Section One]

--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Every weapon in the index has independent psychological research
confirmation. The framework was built from fictional cases; the
psychology was built from real populations using different methods.
Independent convergence across two evidence types confirms structural
patterns are real not fictional artifacts.

Key researchers: Milgram [authority], Bandura [moral disengagement],
  Cialdini [influence], Kahneman/Tversky [cognitive bias], Lifton
  [thought reform], Herman [trauma], Maslach [burnout], Seligman
  [learned helplessness], Tajfel/Turner [social identity], Loftus
  [memory], Haidt [moral psychology], Bowlby [attachment],
  Mullainathan/Shafir [scarcity], Singer [cult psychology].

No weapon in the index lacks psychological confirmation.

==============================================================================

CASES [part of Section Six permanent reference]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Proof for: [what concept this demonstrates]
  Description
  Three-axis [where applicable]
  Weapons active [with quality]
  S: [case strength rating -- see CASE STRENGTH RATING in Section One]

Primary = measurement reference. Used when running readings.
Secondary = only added when variance from primary is substantively
informative. Not different enough = not added.

S-RATING KEY:
  S1 = ILLUSTRATION [fictional, no real-world anchor]
  S2 = WEAK THEORY [fictional with framework-consistent support, OR
       real-world single domain without controlled comparison]
  S3 = CONFIRMED [real-world E3+ AND M3+ AND F2+]
  S4 = STRONG [real-world E4 AND M4 AND F3+]
  Fictional cases cap at S2 regardless of library depth.

PRIMARY CASES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

[CASE:HELL_TAXONOMY]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENERATIVE CYCLE]
Hell taxonomy [Trench Crusade]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Extractive Intrinsic type; compatible selfishness
    producing collective behavior without coordination. Mammon-type
    primary. Collective extraction without coordination requirement.
    Complexity ceiling preventing development beyond intensity scaling.
  Weapons active: none [structural].
  Cross-ref: [REF:HELL_TAXONOMY]

[CASE:BLOOD_SOAKED_COURT]
[PRIMARY_Q:SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY]
Blood-Soaked Court [Trench Crusade]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Axis Substitution [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY]; High coherence
    + High capture simultaneously; Axis-Lock with genuine internal
    experience of captured axis as own values. Scarcity behavior
    negative -- axis stability from external infernal supply chain
    not internal. Three-axis: right, down, back.
  Weapons active: Axis Substitution [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY],
    Legitimacy Transfer [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY].
  Cross-ref: [REF:BLOOD_SOAKED_COURT] -- contrast [REF:CHURCH_MILITANT].

[CASE:CHURCH_MILITANT]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Church Militant [Trench Crusade]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: High coherence under sustained scarcity; same-setting
    contrast with [REF:BLOOD_SOAKED_COURT]. Scarcity behavior
    positive. Most useful same-setting contrast pair in library for
    demonstrating capture axis independence.
    Three-axis: right, up, front-mid.
  Weapons active [defensive]: Obligation Manufacture [MEASUREMENT
    INTEGRITY], Correction Theatre [CORRECTION CAPACITY].
  Cross-ref: [REF:CHURCH_MILITANT]

[CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]
[PRIMARY_Q:SURPLUS PRODUCTION]
Imperium of Man [Warhammer 40K]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Consumption at civilizational scale; substrate
    consumption invisible from inside own measurement surface.
    Net transfer: tithe, conscription, psyker collection extracting
    at accelerating rate while generative capacity declines across
    ten-thousand-year window. SUBSTRATE REGENERATION/SURPLUS
    DISTRIBUTION/CORRECTION TARGETING/OUTPUT INTEGRATION/SIGNAL
    READING/CAUSAL MEMORY all failing simultaneously -- most
    comprehensive extraction case in library.
    Three-axis: right of center, strongly down, mid.
  Weapons active: Normalcy Anchor [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY],
    Horizon Extension [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY], Sacrifice Frame
    [SURPLUS PRODUCTION], Legitimacy Transfer [TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY].
  POLITICAL READING SPLIT [v15]:
    Right reads Imperium as legitimate by survival: 10,000 years
    under total external pressure = smart extraction with floor.
    Left reads as dystopia: suffering exists therefore system
    producing it is primary obstacle. TENSION DISSOLUTION failure
    -- utopian ending requires eliminating the tension that made
    the system real [False Coherence by design].
    Framework: Imperium scores low on almost every quality but
    survives because SR maintained and GC not fully collapsed.
    Smart extraction under total external pressure -- stupid
    extraction would have finished it centuries ago.
  Cross-ref: [REF:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]

[CASE:WORD_BEARERS]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
Word Bearers [Warhammer 40K]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Change-axis Lock; Fertile Lock institutional;
    Transmission Capture [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY];
    self-sustaining configuration. Pre-Monarchia: genuine High
    coherence. Monarchia censure: capture entry. Post-Monarchia:
    examination capacity running at full power justifying fixed
    conclusion. Isstvan III: purging correctable not disloyal.
    Most stable Chaos Legion because configuration is
    self-sustaining. Three-axis: far right, strongly down, back.
  Weapons active: Enantiodromia [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY], Transmission
    Capture [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY], Calibration Strip
    [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY].
  Cross-ref: [REF:WORD_BEARERS]

[CASE:ERGO_PROXY]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]
