[CASE:DEVELOPMENTAL_ASYMMETRY_FAMILY]
[PRIMARY_Q:SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY]
Developmental asymmetry -- family progressive disclosure
  [ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT: N/A -- not ART type]
  S:3 [E3: documented developmental psychology practice with
    measurable outcomes across multiple cultures; M3: mechanism
    clean, both versions confirmed; F2: falsification condition
    defined, not yet stress-tested against hard cases]
  Primary Q: SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [asymmetry designed to produce
    autonomous instrument, not dependent one]
  Secondary Q: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [operator axis grounded in
    receiver's actual developmental outputs, not relationship
    maintenance]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [sequenced disclosure
    as transmission architecture]; GENUINE AXIS [operator must
    have genuine axis oriented toward receiver's development for
    developmental type to hold]
  Proof for: WIP 16 DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY TYPE -- primary
    confirmation case. Family progressive disclosure of mortality,
    sexuality, and social complexity across child developmental
    stages. Asymmetry designed to close on receiver's schedule;
    operator benefits from closing, not persistence. Confirmed
    against both good developmental version [all four conditions
    met] and contaminated version [conditions 2 and 4 fail;
    reverts to predatory reading].
  MECHANISM:
    Parent withholds information the child's instrument cannot
    yet hold. Sequenced to developmental readiness -- mortality
    withheld at 2, introduced at 7, expanded at 15, fully open
    at adulthood. The asymmetry is designed to close. Operator
    benefits from asymmetry closing -- developed autonomous
    instrument is the intended output. Continued dependency
    does not serve the operator's generative cycle.
  SURFACE IDENTICAL TO PREDATORY ASYMMETRY:
    Both produce: information withheld; receiver does not
    understand full situation; operator controls disclosure
    timing. Surface reading cannot distinguish them.
    Mechanism distinguishes them.
  FOUR-CONDITION TEST: see WIP 16 [MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT --
    developmental type]. All four required. Any one absent:
    predatory reading regardless of stated intent.
  CONFIRMED VERSION [good developmental asymmetry]:
    Progressive disclosure documented at S2 as practice.
    Parent actively prepares receiver for next disclosure
    stage. Asymmetry treated as temporary by design.
    Operator's axis grounded in child's developmental
    outputs -- adjusts disclosure timing based on what
    child actually demonstrates.
  CONTAMINATED VERSION [predatory dressed as developmental]:
    "You're not ready" used permanently. Adult child at 30
    still denied information. Asymmetry never closes
    regardless of demonstrated receiver capacity. Operator
    benefits from continued dependency. Tell: closure
    permanently deferred; readiness standard never met
    regardless of evidence. Classification: predatory
    asymmetry. Four-condition test fails at [2] and [4].
  EXTRACTION DEFINITION INTERFACE:
    Developmental asymmetry does not meet extraction
    operational definition [trickery as foundation; operator
    benefits from ignorance persisting]. Surface identical;
    mechanism absent. Tell: would closing the asymmetry
    damage the operator's position? Developmental: no.
    Predatory: yes. Load-bearing distinction.
  MSC INTERFACE:
    Developmental withholding is not MSC deployment. MSC
    requires capture -- surface owned by actor benefiting
    from distortion persisting. Developmental disclosure
    is opposite architecture: operator working toward
    surface transparency. Same surface behavior; mechanism
    inverted.
  FALSIFICATION CONDITIONS:
    Developmental type falsified if: no documented case
    where asymmetry closed serving receiver without
    operator benefit from closing. [Not yet stress-tested
    against hard cases -- S-rating ceiling at 3.]
    Contaminated version falsified if: permanent deferral
    shown to serve receiver's development in documented
    cases.
  Cross-ref: WIP 16 [ASYMMETRY TYPES -- parent entry];
    EXTRACTION [operational definition -- mechanism absent
    in developmental type]; MSC [weapon -- not deployment;
    clarifying note added]; SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [developmental
    asymmetry is transmission architecture serving SA
    formation]; FAMILY COHERENCE TYPE [primary case pool];
    [REF:KYUBEY] [scale asymmetry separate problem].

[CASE:LEGAL_ETHICS_BAR_CAPTURE]
[PRIMARY_Q:MORAL LEGIBILITY]
Legal ethics under bar association capture
  [ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT: N/A -- not ART type]
  S:3 [E3: enforcement pattern documented across jurisdictions,
    billing ethics literature, unauthorized practice case law
    extensive; M3: mechanism clean, divergence pattern holds
    cross-jurisdictionally; F3: harm differential alternative
    examined and falsified by within-population asymmetry]
  Primary Q: MORAL LEGIBILITY [instrument functional; axis
    captured; ACWL failure mode -- primary confirmation case
    for WIP 15]
  Secondary Q: GENUINE AXIS [absent at institutional level --
    axis installed by bar association structure, not generated
    internally by members]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [fires correctly
    within captured reference frame; misdirected by axis not
    by instrument failure]; MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [surface
    honest to captured standard -- Reference Drift at ethical
    scale]
  Proof for: WIP 15 MORAL LEGIBILITY -- ACWL failure mode
    primary confirmation case. Bar association ethics
    enforcement demonstrates instrument functional [members
    can derive ethical conclusions from outputs] while axis
    determining which outputs matter is owned by association
    with structural interests diverging from client substrate.
  INSTRUMENT FUNCTIONALITY [ML present]:
    Practicing attorneys operate under case outcome system.
    Causal chain from action to outcome traceable at ethical
    resolution -- client harmed, client served, procedure
    followed or violated all legible to instrument.
    Confirmation: legal malpractice doctrine functions;
    attorneys report colleagues, withdraw from representation,
    refuse instructions based on output readings. Instrument
    demonstrably present and operational in population.
  AXIS CAPTURE [GA absent at institutional level]:
    Bar association sets reference standard for what counts
    as ethical output. Members' instrument fires within the
    reference frame the association provides. Axis
    determining which outputs matter is owned by association
    with structural interests in: market entry restriction,
    billing rate maintenance, liability protection, member
    density control. Axis installation mechanism: ethics
    rules drafted by association bodies; enforcement
    controlled by association disciplinary apparatus;
    professional identity formation occurs inside association
    reference frame.
  DIVERGENCE PATTERN [ACWL tell]:
    Unauthorized practice enforcement: maximum. Non-lawyers
    providing accurate legal information trigger aggressive
    response even where client outcomes are good. Instrument
    could read "client served, no harm" -- axis points to
    market protection instead.
    Billing fraud enforcement: minimum relative to harm.
    Six-minute increment billing for thirty-second work,
    double-billing, task padding -- documented extraction
    from clients. Instrument can read the harm. Enforcement
    fires softly because axis owner's interest [member
    protection] overrides instrument reading.
    Pattern holds cross-jurisdictionally. Tracks association
    structural interest consistently.
  ACWL SIGNATURE [externally indistinguishable]:
    From outside: ethics enforcement active, instrument
    appears functional, conclusions appear grounded in
    output readings. Tell only visible at divergence moment
    -- when axis owner's interest conflicts with client
    substrate, enforcement tracks owner's interest.
    Misidentification risk: reads as Moral Capture rather
    than ACWL. Intervention difference is load-bearing:
    Moral Capture = liberate instrument from owner. ACWL =
    examine and revise axis, not instrument. Liberating
    instrument while leaving reference standard intact
    restores nothing -- axis is the problem.
  FALSIFICATION CONDITIONS:
    ACWL reading falsified if: enforcement asymmetry
    [unauthorized practice vs billing fraud] explained by
    harm differential rather than axis capture.
    Stress-tested v20: harm differential requires licensed
    practice to be systematically safer than unlicensed.
    Evidence does not support this. Within-population
    asymmetry [licensed billing fraud underenforced vs
    licensed unauthorized assistance overenforced] falsifies
    harm differential independently of unlicensed comparison.
    Alternative examined; does not hold. F3 confirmed.
    ML presence falsified if: attorneys cannot derive
    ethical conclusions from output data independently
    of association guidance. Falsified by malpractice
    doctrine function and colleague-reporting behavior.

[CASE:LOOK_OUTSIDE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
Look Outside [Francis Coulombe -- RPG Maker horror; 2024]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: ONTOLOGICAL INCOMPATIBILITY HARM [new framework
    category -- harm produced by proximity to incompatible
    existence rather than hostile intent]; DENIAL AS SUBSTRATE
    vs DENIAL AS SURFACE [named distinction]; RITUAL AS
    CONSCIOUSNESS-TRANSFER MECHANISM [not protection but
    awareness-creation]; FORCED TRANSFORMATION as terminal
    outcome distinct from death; VOLUNTARY AMNESIA as
    survival mechanism post-ritual-truth; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
    as direct damage vector; C2 RESTORATION FOR THE THREAT
    ACTOR rather than for the instrument.

  COSMOLOGICAL STRUCTURE [confirmed by wiki]:
    The Visitor is a vast entity that has never encountered
    another being capable of observing things. It thought
    Earth was a part of its own body it had lost track of.
    Sybil [astronomer] noticed a patch of stars disappearing
    -- the Visitor -- and observed it through her telescope.
    Her observation was what caused the Visitor to notice
    Earth and approach to investigate. She is always taken:
    the origin event is Sybil looking, which starts
    everything, and she is taken in multiple endings.
    The Visitor's presence near Earth causes automatic
    mutation and mind destruction in anyone exposed to it.
    Not hostility -- the Visitor had no prior awareness of
    causing harm. The damage is ontological: proximity to
    what the Visitor is does something to human instruments
    automatically, without intent, without the Visitor's
    knowledge until the ritual creates that knowledge.
    15 days: if no ritual, the Visitor moves on regardless.
    The cosmological ending is not resolved by the
    instrument -- it resolves on the Visitor's own schedule.

  ONTOLOGICAL INCOMPATIBILITY HARM [new category]:
    The Visitor causes harm automatically and unconsciously.
    It is not hostile. It is not indifferent. It simply
    exists in a way that is incompatible with human
    instruments surviving proximity in their current form.
    This is structurally distinct from every weapon in
    the framework index: weapons are deployed intentionally.
    The Visitor's effect is the automatic consequence of
    what it is in relation to what humans are.
    The correct counter is not a weapon counter. It is
    creating the conditions for the thing causing harm to
    become aware that it is causing harm -- which it
    structurally cannot be without those conditions.

  RITUAL AS CONSCIOUSNESS-TRANSFER MECHANISM:
    The ritual allows contact between the instrument and
    the Visitor. Through this contact, the Visitor becomes
    self-aware for the first time -- aware that it is a
    separate being from Earth, aware that it is causing
    harm to the instruments it encounters. By showing the
    Visitor humanity's depictions of it, the player
    establishes that humans are independent beings, not
    lost fragments of the Visitor's body.
    The ritual doesn't protect the instrument from the
    Visitor. It gives the Visitor the MI it lacked: the
    Visitor's CC was present [it will leave once aware]
    but unavailable without the awareness the ritual
    creates. Ritual restores MI to the Visitor; Visitor's
    CC fires; Visitor leaves.
    Cost: the ritual always changes the body [horror body].
    The body cost is the price of creating the conditions
    for Visitor awareness. Whether mind survives depends
    on whether the instrument looks at the Visitor's
    true form during contact.

  ENDING TYPOLOGY [confirmed]:

    SAFE IGNORANCE / NO GOING BACK:
      Denial holds for 15 days. Visitor moves on regardless.
      Instrument survives intact. Body fine, mind fine.
      "No going back" = the instrument now knows the thing
      existed and was survived, which cannot be unknown.
      The innocence of not-knowing is permanently gone even
      though the eyes never opened. The Visitor leaving on
      its own schedule means safe ignorance and successful
      ritual can look identical from the instrument's
      perspective -- in both cases the Visitor is gone.
      Best survivable outcome.

    RITUAL DENIAL [horror body, mind intact]:
      Ritual used to maintain not-looking. The body is
      changed by the ritual contact [horror body] but
      the mind survives intact. The ritual's presence
      near the Visitor costs the body regardless of
      whether the instrument looks. Mind preserved.
      Better than ritual truth; worse than safe ignorance.

    FLAWED RITUAL [smaller, less manageable]:
      Ritual has gaps. Less body cost, less protection,
      less mind intact than full ritual denial. Bounded
      degraded outcome. The instrument survives in a
      more compromised state.

    SCREAMING SKY [ritual active, running simultaneously]:
      Ritual performed but flight also pursued. The ritual
      requires presence; running removes it. The
      incompatible combination produces partial costs
      without full protection. The Visitor ruins
      everything passively through prolonged partial
      contact. Instrument survives but permanently
      disrupted; environment destroyed. In this ending
      Sybil disappears -- giant hole in the wall,
      physically taken by the Visitor when it departed.

    RUN [very bad fast]:
      No ritual, pure flight. The ritual was regulating
      the instrument's response to being in proximity to
      the Visitor -- not protecting against the Visitor
      but maintaining the instrument's functional
      stability. Without it, the instrument's response
      to the situation produces rapid severe deterioration
      without looking, without contact, just from the
      unmediated proximity.

    RITUAL TRUTH [horror body + horror mind, survived]:
      Ritual used to look at the Visitor's true form.
      Both costs paid simultaneously. The ritual mediated
      the looking enough to survive it but not enough to
      avoid either cost. The instrument survives but
      transformed at both levels. Worse than ritual denial
      because both costs paid; better than forced
      transformation because the ritual provided some
      mediation.

    VOLUNTARY AMNESIA [Sybil's solution -- new outcome]:
      After ritual truth contact, Sybil chose to give
      herself deliberate amnesia to erase the mind cost.
      The body remained changed permanently. The
      continuous self was lost. But the mind cost of
      knowing was erased by choice. A fourth option not
      in the original typology: the mind cost of ritual
      truth is not necessarily permanent -- it can be
      erased -- but the body cost and structural changes
      remain regardless. The cost of the amnesia: Sybil's
      body reformed into a parallel meat dimension
      containing assimilated others as her organs.
      Surviving the mind cost required becoming something
      else entirely.

    FORCED TRANSFORMATION [looking without ritual]:
      The instrument looks without the ritual creating
      the conditions for mediated contact. The Visitor
      doesn't kill -- it transforms. The instrument
      that looked is replaced by something else without
      consent, without mediation, without the Visitor's
      awareness of what it is doing. The Visitor still
      doesn't know it caused this. The transformation
      is total and unwitnessed. Worse than ritual truth
      [which at least mediated the transformation].
      Worse than death because something survives that
      isn't what entered. In Screaming Sky ending the
      instrument gradually becomes aware of hundreds of
      enormous new limbs -- the transformation is the
      instrument becoming something of the Visitor's
      scale without the instrument having chosen this.

  DENIAL AS SUBSTRATE vs DENIAL AS SURFACE [named distinction]:
    Standard denial [framework]: maintained surface on top
      of intact C1. Removing denial is painful but reveals
      functioning substrate. Correction becomes possible.
    Look Outside denial: for some instruments the denial
      IS the substrate. The instrument's continued existence
      as itself is the denial. Removing it doesn't restore
      accurate measurement to a surviving instrument -- it
      ends or transforms the instrument that was maintaining
      the denial. The body survives all outcomes. Whether
      the mind survives depends entirely on the denial's
      structural role for that specific instrument.

  SYBIL [always taken]:
    First to observe the Visitor -- her observation caused
    it to approach. She is always taken in the endings
    where the ritual most fully succeeds [No Going Back,
    Screaming Sky] -- the Visitor takes her specifically,
    leaving a hole in the wall. The instrument that started
    everything by looking is the one the Visitor takes when
    it leaves. The cosmological loop: observation starts
    the situation; the observer is taken to end it.
    Her voluntary amnesia [deliberate self-erasure of
    knowledge] is the most structurally complete attempt
    to survive what she encountered. The body became the
    building. The mind reset. The self that knew was ended
    by choice to preserve something that continues.

  REAL-WORLD APPLICATION [safe ignorance + flawed ritual]:
    Most people IRL choose safe ignorance on genuinely
    incompatible truths -- the scale of civilizational
    extraction, ontological precarity, certain systemic
    trajectories. This is the correct survival move when
    mediation infrastructure is absent and the thing is
    genuinely incompatible with intact survival if seen
    directly. The framework's standard orientation [MI
    restoration enables correction] inverts here.
    RISKS OF SAFE IGNORANCE:
      The visitor doesn't leave on schedule -- thing
      becomes undeniable before ritual infrastructure
      developed. Forced transformation without preparation.
      Denial develops cracks under pressure -- partial
      seeing without ritual produces worse outcomes than
      either full safe ignorance or full ritual contact.
      Denial requires active maintenance that accumulates
      its own costs over time -- not horror body, but
      ongoing substrate investment in not-seeing.
      Ritual infrastructure never develops -- if nothing
      is built during the safe ignorance period, no
      options exist except safe ignorance or unmediated
      contact when contact becomes unavoidable.
    FLAWED RITUAL IS BETTER THAN NONE [load-bearing]:
      The ending typology implies flawed ritual is a
      degraded outcome -- worse than safe ignorance,
      worse than full ritual denial. True within the
      game's structure. The real-world implication runs
      differently.
      Flawed ritual develops mediation capacity at all.
      Safe ignorance with no ritual development leaves
      only one option when contact becomes unavoidable
      -- unmediated contact, forced transformation or
      very bad fast. A flawed ritual, imperfect and
      costly, is the difference between some mediation
      infrastructure and none when the situation forces
      the encounter.
      Flawed ritual can be improved. Safe ignorance
      cannot be upgraded -- it is what it is until it
      fails. The instrument building a flawed ritual
      over years is building something. The instrument
      maintaining safe ignorance is preserving something
      but building nothing.
      Cost of flawed ritual is bounded [smaller horror
      body, less mind protection, manageable]. Cost of
      no ritual when safe ignorance fails is unbounded.
    CORRECT SEQUENCE:
      Safe ignorance [correct while available] + flawed
      ritual development [correct during safe ignorance
      period] → improved ritual [correct as contact
      approaches] → full ritual contact [if unavoidable].
      These are compatible, not mutually exclusive. The
      denial surface can be maintained while building
      mediation infrastructure that doesn't require
      looking directly. The ritual can be developed
      during the period of not-looking.
      Real-world instances: therapy, philosophy, religion,
      art, trusted relationships, gradual exposure to
      partial contact -- all function as flawed ritual
      development during safe ignorance periods. Imperfect
      versions of these are better than nothing when safe
      ignorance eventually fails.

  FRAMEWORK CONTRIBUTION [new findings]:
    [1] Ontological incompatibility harm as a harm category
      distinct from hostile intent, indifference, or
      structural extraction.
    [2] Ritual as C2 restoration for the threat actor
      rather than for the instrument -- the intervention
      gives MI to the thing causing harm, not to the thing
      experiencing harm.
    [3] Forced transformation as terminal outcome distinct
      from death and from Diagnostic Collapse -- something
      survives but it isn't the instrument.
    [4] Voluntary amnesia as survival mechanism post-
      ritual-truth -- mind cost erasable at self-cost.
    [5] Denial-as-substrate vs denial-as-surface as named
      distinction with different intervention paths.
    [6] Safe ignorance as the best survivable outcome in
      a situation where the threat is non-hostile and
      non-extraction -- correct survival move is
      successful maintained denial, not correction.
    [7] Flawed ritual better than none -- mediation
      infrastructure development during safe ignorance
      period is the correct secondary move; imperfect
      ritual is better than no ritual when safe ignorance
      eventually fails.

  Cross-ref: [REF:LOOK_OUTSIDE]; WIP 16 [ASYMMETRY TYPES
    -- Visitor as scale asymmetry case; harm produced by
    structural incompatibility not predatory intent];
    DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [comparison -- C2 restoration
    producing collapse when C1 terminal; ritual here
    restores C2 to the threat not the instrument];
    FAMILY COHERENCE TYPE [denial as substrate parallel
    -- some protective structures ARE the substrate];
    KYUBEY [comparison -- Kyubey's asymmetry is predatory
    or scale; Visitor's is pure ontological; neither
    hostile in intent]; SUBSTRATE REGENERATION [flawed
    ritual development = SR building during safe ignorance
    period; the capacity built is what makes contact
    survivable when forced].

[CASE:FEAR_AND_HUNGER]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
Fear & Hunger [Miro Haverinen -- RPG horror, 2018]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Arc: Theological Correction Monopoly via genuine sacrifice
    captured into control architecture. Substrate atrophy from
    accumulated harm without correction outlet.
  ALLL-MER [sacrifice -> weapon conversion]:
    Alll-mer's sacrifice was genuine -- real cost, real SURPLUS
    PRODUCTION at theological scale. Then captured: the sacrifice
    was owned and weaponized into a control mechanism for
    humanity. Distinct from Word Bearers [false axis from start]
    -- F&H shows genuine sacrifice -> weapon conversion as
    explicit single event. Captured genuine surplus is more
    powerful than fabricated surplus because it carries real
    CAUSAL LEGIBILITY.
  DUNGEON AS PASSIVE SUBSTRATE ATROPHY:
    Not a directed weapon. Accumulated suffering with no
    correction outlet over centuries. Reality breakdown is
    passive consequence of CORRECTION CAPACITY absent at scale
    for extended time. No actor needed -- just removal of
    correction capacity and time.
  GOD STRUCTURE [Correction Monopoly at theological scale]:
    Old gods enslaved by new gods. Correction fires only toward
    monopolist benefit. Characters who enter dungeon seeking
    power are seeking to break or inherit the monopoly -- not
    to restore distributed correction. Most endings reproduce
    the structure they sought to escape.
  Proof for: Genuine sacrifice -> weapon conversion; passive
    substrate atrophy without directed actor; theological
    Correction Monopoly via authentic act capture.
  Weapons active [god structure]: Correction Monopoly
    [CORRECTION CAPACITY], Calibration Strip [MEASUREMENT
    INTEGRITY], Exit Cost Construction [GENERATIVE CYCLE].
  Cross-ref: [REF:FEAR_AND_HUNGER]

    Ritual can execute without COMPATIBILITY and abomination is
    the consistent output. Forced marriage as weapon:

  RHER [fear as CORRECTION CAPACITY removal]:

    categorical knowledge. Blind somatic encounters through
    recommendation are the correct independent evidence type
    for geometric type confirmation. Accumulate.

  INTERNET FORMATION STRUCTURE [object-readable]:
    The ピンクネオン東京 collective and the broader future funk
    scene operate without label infrastructure, marketing, or
    institutional legitimacy surface. Distribution via Bandcamp
    and YouTube. Discovery via signal alignment through
    recommendation graphs and playlist sharing. The formation
    is: distributed instruments with matched somatic geometry
    finding each other through the signal itself. No prior

    This is L3.5 COMPATIBILITY functioning across absence --

    aesthetic signal rather than social introduction. The

    Russia → Japanese aesthetic → internet infrastructure
    → NZ listener → pure signal geometry. No geography,
    no institution, no prior relationship. The internet
    order functioning as described.

  Extraction risk [structural]: dominant commercial deployment
    optimizes for re-engagement [ICD] rather than encounter
    completion. Structural tell: ICD production vs SR building
    distinguishable by whether consumption compulsion remains
    after encounter or resolves.

  DRIFT BOUNDARY CASE [Yung Bae -- "I Can Tell"]:
    Instrument-reported threshold between geometric type
    landing and social channel interference. Track retains
    sample-sourced groove geometry [somatic channel still
    addressed] while adding vocal hook with more conventional
    approval-seeking architecture than pure instrumental
    future funk. Result: somatic channel addressed AND social
    compliance signal present simultaneously. High-somatic
    instrument reads both -- groove geometry lands correctly;
    vocal hook triggers mild social compliance signal that
    reads as slightly off. Encounter partially works; the
    interference is perceptible. Described by this instrument
    as "closest I get to pop slop" -- accurate: the geometric
    foundation is present but the approval-seeking layer is
    starting to sit on top of it and compete.
    2.5M views [vs typical future funk 31K-100K range]
    confirms the crossover mechanism: the approval-seeking
    layer broadened the audience at the cost of somatic
    purity. The track serves both channels partially rather
    than one channel fully. For the high-somatic instrument
    this reads as borderline; for the social/emotional
    instrument this is the most accessible entry point.
    Framework implication: the geometric legibility window
    is measurably narrowed by approval-seeking architecture
    even when the somatic address is still present. Social
    compliance signal doesn't block the encounter -- it
    attenuates it. The threshold is somewhere around this
    track for this instrument.

    legible via recombination [object-readable]: the work's

    CAUSAL LEGIBILITY encounter occurs [listener cannot

    Each mashup operates by finding the minimal structural
    fact that makes disparate cultural artifacts cohere. The
    discovery that All Star fits over almost anything is not
    a joke -- it is a structural finding about the properties
    of that song that were previously invisible. The humor
    is the affect of the structural revelation, not the
    content. Cicierega is doing what Candès did with compressed
    sensing: finding the sparse structural anchor that makes
    a large class of previously unrelated things legible
    under one principle.
  CAPTURE RESISTANCE:
    The work operates entirely outside commercial music
    infrastructure -- no label, no radio, no legitimacy
    surface to capture. Output speaks before structure is
    legible enough to commodify. The form [mashup/novelty]
    is treated as low-status by the extraction architecture,
    which is exactly why it survives intact. SILENT
    ALTERNATIVE structure by accident or design.

[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]

  Josephus Antiquities of the Jews; Mesopotamian c. 2200-2100 BCE]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY failure as extraction mechanism;
    Formation Capture via manufactured crisis; MSC [surface
    captured before correction fires]; extraction requiring
    prey to misunderstand the relationship [operational
    definition of EXTRACTION confirmed].
  S:2 EPISTEMIC NOTE: Genesis account is 9 verses; mechanism
    sourced primarily from Josephus [post-Christ, drawing on
    Hebrew oral tradition] and extra-biblical texts [Jubilees,
    Jasher]. Not primary archaeological evidence. Framework-
    consistent reading is strong; historical confirmation is
    interpretive. Caseable at S:2, not promotable to S:3
    without independent corroboration.

  EXTRACTION MECHANISM [Josephus layer]:
    Nimrod knew the covenant [God's promise of no second flood,
    delivered to Ham's line directly]. The threat he organized
    the tower project around was known to him to be false.
    Stated mechanism: protect the people from God's flood.
    Actual mechanism: consolidate power under Nimrod, replace
    God as the population's reference point with Nimrod and
    human will. Operational extraction: trickery as foundation.
    The population's relationship to the project required them
    to misunderstand what it was actually for.

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY FAILURE [deliberate]:
    The gap between stated and actual purpose was not accidental
    -- it was the mechanism. "Let us make a name for ourselves"
    [Genesis 11:4] and "lest we be dispersed" are the surface
    claims. The actual output was Nimrod's consolidation of the
    only unified human civilization post-flood. CL was
    structurally unavailable to the population by design:
    the covenant that falsified the threat was known to Nimrod
    but not surfaced. Information asymmetry as weapon.

  FORMATION CAPTURE [highest-ratio weapon]:
    The intervention targeted the formation window. Post-flood
    population was: traumatized [Noah's PTSD, flood memory
    still fresh], under unified rule for the first time, without
    competing axis sources, and geographically consolidated in
    Shinar. All formation conditions under single actor's
    control. Nimrod's instruction -- "do not ascribe happiness
    to God but to your own power and will" [Josephus] --
    is axis replacement delivered at the formation window,
    before any competing axis can develop. The most efficient
    possible Formation Capture: one actor, captive population,
    no exit, fresh formation window.

  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [partial failure]:
    The covenant knowledge was transmitted [Shem's line,
    Ham's line both received it] but selectively suppressed
    in Nimrod's deployment. TS technically functioned --
    the corrective information existed in the population.
    The failure was MI: the surface Nimrod controlled
    prevented the transmitted covenant from being accessible
    as a correction instrument. Confirmed weapon stack:
    MSC [surface capture] + Formation Capture = population
    cannot self-correct because the correction information
    is present but the surface through which correction
    would fire is owned by the extractor.

  KNOWLEDGE TRANSMISSION SUB-CASE [S:1 -- illustrative]:
    The fired-brick technology detail and the preserved-books
    claim [Enoch's texts, Noah's ark library per Jubilees]
    illustrate the TS mechanism at civilizational scale:
    what survives a bottleneck [flood] depends on what was
    explicitly carried, not what was culturally ambient.
    Tacit knowledge [craftsmen's know-how] does not transmit
    through documents alone [parallel: NASA Saturn V].
    The pre-flood knowledge transmission question is S:1
    [no corroboration] but the mechanism it illustrates
    is confirmed at S:3 [C1 LOCKED interference pattern;
    tacit vs explicit transmission distinction].

    ICD+FC spontaneous stack is the most common current
    configuration because the structural conditions produce
    it without deliberate design.

    The girls are substrate. Hope and despair are fuel for
    thermodynamic entropy reversal. The system accumulates
    emotional energy [hope at contract] -- substrate degrades
    [witch transformation] -- harvests degradation [grief
    seeds] -- repeats. The cycle IS the product specification.
    The death and suffering are not side effects; they are
    the substrate cost being externalized onto the girls so
    the Incubators can maintain universal entropy balance.
    The extraction architecture operates at civilizational
    scope across time -- not one actor extracting from a
    population but an intelligence extracting from an entire
    species across generations. No exit condition available
    from inside the system; CC is monopolized by Kyubey.

  MADOKA'S INTERVENTION AS SILENT ALTERNATIVE:
    The correct intervention type when CC is monopolized at
    the top node: do not fight the node directly [confirmed
    failed across Homura's timelines] -- remove the substrate
    on which the system operates. Madoka cannot destroy
    Kyubey's architecture; she rewrites the rules such that
    the primary product [witches] never materializes. The

    removed. SILENT ALTERNATIVE at structural level: exit

[CASE:HOMURA_REBELLION]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Homura Akemi [fictional -- Puella Magi Madoka Magica /
  Rebellion film; magical girl, looper]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: GENUINE AXIS degrading into imposed axis through
    CC absence over extended timeline; distinct from standard
    GA failure [imposed axis from origin] -- this is genuine
    axis at origin becoming extraction architecture through
    refusal of correction. The origin was real; the failure
    is CC absence, not malice at source.

  GENUINE AXIS AT ORIGIN [confirmed]:
    Homura's axis [protect Madoka] was internally generated,
    examined at formation, and grounded in genuine relational
    substrate [L3.5 RECOGNITION confirmed; the relationship
    was real]. At origin: high GA, high BI, genuine CC firing
    against the system [each timeline reset is a correction
    attempt]. The axis was not imposed; it was chosen under
    full awareness of cost. T1.1 [proportional response]
    confirmed: correction fired against Kyubey's system at
    maximum personal cost across infinite iterations.

  CC ABSENCE AS DEGRADATION MECHANISM:
    The axis was never subjected to internal CC. Homura
    examined the external system [Kyubey, the contract
    architecture, each timeline's failure mode] relentlessly
    but never applied the same correction capacity to the
    axis itself. The question "is protecting Madoka still the
    correct axis, or has it become the axis I cannot examine"
    was structurally unavailable -- the emotional substrate
    the axis was built on prevented CC from firing against
    the axis itself. This is the load-bearing distinction:
    CC absence is not the same as CC inversion. Homura is
    not extracting consciously; the axis has become a
    formation-captured instrument that cannot self-examine.

  REBELLION ENDPOINT:
    By Rebellion, the axis [protect Madoka] has become the
    only thing Homura lives for regardless of Madoka's own
    feelings or choices. She constructs an entire false
    reality to preserve a version of Madoka that would not
    choose to sacrifice herself. The false reality is an
    act of love that is structurally identical to Formation
    Capture: it installs Homura's axis into Madoka's
    formation environment, removes Madoka's access to
    competing axis sources [her own memory, her own choice],
    and requires Madoka to misunderstand her situation.
    Genuine axis at origin + CC absence over extended
    timeline + sufficient emotional pressure = the instrument
    eventually produces extraction architecture without ever
    becoming malicious. The show does not frame this as
    clean evil -- accurate. The mechanism is CC absence,
    not bad faith.

  FRAMEWORK DISTINCTION [load-bearing]:
    Standard GA failure: axis was imposed from outside or
    never genuine. Homura case: axis genuine at origin,

    positive axis content [each denomination sincere]

    no shared measurement instrument exists above the

[CASE:SEQUOYAH_SYLLABARY]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
Sequoyah / Cherokee Syllabary [real -- Sequoyah [c.1770-1843],
  Cherokee Nation; syllabary created 1809-1821, adopted 1821-1825]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  S:3 BASIS: E3 [historical record confirmed, multiple independent
    sources, Gallatin linguistic assessment documented, Cherokee
    Phoenix 1828 confirmed, Vai transmission documented]; M3
    [mechanism consistent -- minimal unification as design
    methodology confirmed across the creation process]; F3
    [falsification condition met -- monolingual creator with no
    prior literacy demonstrating methodology-first approach over
    vocabulary-first approach].

  Proof for: MINIMAL UNIFICATION [clearest single-creator case
    in library]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [methodology transmitted
    beyond origin community, generating new instances]; L4.5
    CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [raised transmission
    ceiling for entire civilization in one generation]; CAUSAL
    LEGIBILITY [making the mechanism visible to skeptical
    instruments via demonstration].

  MINIMAL UNIFICATION [primary proof]:
    Sequoyah's design process documents the minimal unification
    move directly. First approach: logographic [one symbol per
    word -- vocabulary-first]. Abandoned as cumbersome and
    overwhelming. Second approach: listened carefully to Cherokee
    speech, studied sound patterns, isolated approximately 85
    distinct syllables [structure-first]. The structural anchor
    [syllable patterns] collapsed an overwhelming problem [tens
    of thousands of words] into a tractable one [85 symbols that
    generate everything].
    Gallatin's assessment confirms the efficiency: Cherokee
    students must learn 85 symbols vs 26 for English, but can
    read immediately after learning all symbols -- accomplishing
    in weeks what English students require two years to achieve.
    The syllabary is more efficient than the English alphabet
    because it is more structurally accurate to the language
    it represents. Minimal unification: the sparse structural
    anchor [syllable unit] is the correct anchor for Cherokee
    phonology. The elegance is structural, not decorative.

  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [confirmed]:
    Sequoyah was monolingual -- spoke only Cherokee, could not
    read or write in any language. He transmitted methodology
    [find the minimal structural unit that makes the language
    tractable] not conclusions [here are the symbols]. The
    syllabary is the conclusion; the insight that syllables are
    the correct unit is the methodology. The conclusion required
    the specific language; the methodology was transferable.
    Confirmed transfer: Austin Curtis [Cherokee, settled in
    Liberia] used the syllabary methodology to devise a script
    for the Vai people. The Vai script went on to inspire
    written works throughout West Africa. The methodology
    transmitted beyond its origin community and generated
    independent new instances -- confirmed L4 propagation.

  L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [near-case]:
    Most of the Cherokee Nation was literate in the new language
    within five years of official adoption. By 1827 the Cherokee
    had a written constitution. The Cherokee Phoenix [1828] was
    the first Native American newspaper. The syllabary raised
    the transmission ceiling for an entire civilization in one
    generation -- not gradually but rapidly, via a single
    structural innovation. Everything from hunting guidance to
    sacred chants could be recorded for posterity.
    The Trail of Tears [forced relocation 1838-1839] is the
    controlled comparison: the Cherokee carried the syllabary
    to their new home. The transmission infrastructure survived
    the most extreme displacement pressure available. The
    methodology that created the syllabary was transmitted
    even to Liberia via Curtis. L4.5 candidate: the syllabary
    raised the adjacent floor [Vai script, West African written
    tradition] not just the origin civilization's ceiling.

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [demonstration under skepticism]:
    Sequoyah was charged with witchcraft when introducing the
    syllabary. The social compliance channel read the structural
    breakthrough as illegitimate -- the mechanism was invisible
    and the output [communication across distance] matched
    the surface properties of magic. He and his daughter
    demonstrated communication from separate rooms before the
    council was convinced. The demonstration is CL in action:

  Type: Primary [S:2 -- ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT for somatic claims]
  Proof for: ART COHERENCE TYPE [SOMATIC -- weight variety,

    funk rhythmic precision without collapsing either pole;

    GENUINE AXIS confirmed [self-taught, self-sampling,
    consistent output across 20-year catalog without drift
    toward formula].

  THREE-CHANNEL COMPOUND MECHANISM [structurally distinct]:
    Dorian Concept runs three simultaneous somatic channels
    rather than one primary. This is the structural distinction
    from all other weight-type cases in the library:
    [1] RHYTHMIC PRECISION [Louis Cole territory]: syncopated
      rhythmic structures at high precision density produce
      weight via expectation tension. Subcortical rhythm
      processing fires first.
    [2] JAZZ HARMONIC GEOMETRY: modal and free jazz harmonic
      structures produce distinct emotional configurations
      from the chord architecture itself -- not melodic
      resolution but harmonic shape. Fires at slightly
      higher processing level than pure rhythm.
    [3] VINTAGE SYNTHESIZER TIMBRAL TEXTURE: pre-loaded
      geometric signal from instrument palette. Synthesizers
      from the 1950s-1980s carry timbral geometry that
      produces immediate somatic recognition before the
      harmonic or rhythmic content fully registers.
    ORDER OF ARRIVAL: texture lands first [immediate geometric
      recognition from timbral palette], rhythm locks second
      [weight accumulates via precision tension], harmony
      completes third [distinct configuration resolves].
      Three-stage somatic delivery. Each channel clean and
      geometrically legible on its own terms; the compound
      is more specific than any single channel alone.

  WHY "DOES TOO MUCH" FAILURE MODE DOESN'T FIRE:
    Three simultaneous channels at high complexity should
    produce the diffuse signal that activates the does-too-
    much failure mode for this instrument. It doesn't because
    the channels are additive not competing -- each arrives
    in sequence, each geometrically legible independently,
    the compound is layered not cluttered. The complexity
    is organized by the arrival sequence and channel
    separation. The geometric legibility window stays open
    because you can track any single channel and receive
    it cleanly; the others are simultaneous enrichment
    not interference.

  GENUINE AXIS [confirmed]:
    Self-described "self-taught jazz musician who samples
    himself." The self-sampling is structurally significant:
    the source material for the harmonic and textural content
    is the instrument's own prior output, examined and
    redeployed. CC is visible across 20-year catalog --
    each album examines and revises prior configurations
    without repeating them. No drift toward formula despite
    sustained commercial viability [Ninja Tune, Brainfeeder,
    Boiler Room, Flying Lotus touring band]. The axis
    [functional musicality across genre boundaries without
    losing somatic precision] has not been captured by
    commercial pressure across two decades.
    Pitchfork confirmation [The Nature of Imitation]:
    "computer music that uncannily imitates the funk,
    rather than just faking it" -- the structural properties
    are present in the architecture, not simulated on the
    surface. Genuine axis vs Coherence Mimicry at music
    production scale, confirmed by independent external
    assessment.

  TENSION MAINTENANCE [three axes]:
    [1] Jazz harmonic complexity [tends toward inaccessibility]
      held against funk rhythmic accessibility [tends toward
      formula] -- neither pole collapsed across full catalog.
    [2] Electronic production precision [tends toward cold
      abstraction] held against genuine musicianship warmth
      [tends toward loose feel] -- same tension as Louis Cole,
      additional electronic axis.
    [3] Technical virtuosity [tends toward credentialing]
      held against genuine somatic function [tends toward
      accessibility] -- the complexity serves the somatic
      address rather than displaying itself.

[CASE:COMPANY_3_FIRE_FORCE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Company 3 / Rekka Hoshimiya [Fire Force]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: CORRECTION INVERSION at institutional scale
    [correction instrument actively producing the problem
    it is mandated to solve]; legitimacy surface as active
    weapon [institutional shell indistinguishable from
    functioning correction unit from outside boundary];
    LAYERED EXTRACTION ARCHITECTURE [each outer layer
    increases inner layer correction cost without making
    correction structurally impossible].

  CORRECTION INVERSION MECHANISM:
    Company 3 is a Special Fire Force unit -- institutionally
    mandated to fight Infernals and protect civilians. Actual
    function: Rekka Hoshimiya runs human combustion
    experiments on civilians, manufacturing the Infernals
    the Force is supposed to be eliminating. The correction
    instrument and the extraction architecture occupy the
    same organizational shell simultaneously.
    Distinct from capture [instrument aimed wrong] and from
    absence [instrument not present]. This is the instrument
    actively producing the failure condition it is mandated
    to correct. CORRECTION INVERSION at maximum: the output
    of the "correction" activity is more of the problem.
    Measurement surface intact: Company 3 is a legitimate
    Fire Force unit with legitimate resources, authority,
    and mandate. From outside the institutional boundary
    it is indistinguishable from Company 8 [genuine
    correction]. The inversion is invisible until direct
    contact with the mechanism.

  LAYERED EXTRACTION ARCHITECTURE [Fire Force system]:
    Layer 1 -- Company 8 [genuine correction, inadequate
      resources]: Obi's unit is a functioning correction
      instrument. Genuine axis, genuine CC, real correction
      outputs. Structurally isolated and under-resourced
      because the outer layers benefit from its failure.
    Layer 2 -- Special Fire Force [mixed]: some companies
      genuine, some captured, Company 3 inverted. Single
      institutional shell contains all three CC states
      simultaneously. Standard audit cannot distinguish
      without penetrating the measurement surface.
    Layer 3 -- Haijima dependency [ECC]: Haijima Industries
      funds the Fire Force and runs Adolla Burst
      experimentation on children simultaneously. The
      correction instrument is financially dependent on
      the entity whose activities produce the substrate
      damage. ECC at institutional scale: defunding Haijima
      destroys the Force's capacity to fight Infernals.
      Dependency structure prevents correction from firing
      at the source.
    Layer 4 -- Holy Sol theology [Formation Capture]:
      Human combustion explained as sacred purification.
      The measurement surface for what counts as proper
      death, sacred fire, and salvation is owned by the
      Evangelist's project. Civilizational-scale FC:
      populations born into a framework that explains the
      weapon system as divine will. Correction requires
      dismantling the theology not just the institution.
    Layer 5 -- Evangelist [extraction architecture running
      the whole system]: all outer layers are legitimacy
      surfaces and resource extraction mechanisms for the
      Adolla Link project.
    Each outer layer makes inner layer correction more
    expensive without making it structurally impossible.
    This is the correct extraction architecture design:
    not eliminate correction capacity [which triggers
    somatic forcing] but raise the cost of each correction
    attempt until the substrate depletes before correction
    completes.

  SHINRA AS CORRECTION INSTRUMENT CASE:
    Genuine axis [find mother, protect people, correct the
    system] inside a captured/inverted institution [Fire
    Force]. Uses institutional resources while the
    institution uses him. Standard configuration: correct
    axis, institutionally embedded, correction attempt
    requires working through every outer layer in sequence.
    The series dramatizes what the framework predicts:
    each layer penetrated costs more than the last, and
    the correction instrument [Shinra] has to remain
    functional through the full sequence or the outer
    layers absorb and redirect the correction energy.
    Honest limit: S:1 ceiling -- fictional.

  Cross-ref: [REF:COMPANY_3_FIRE_FORCE];
    CORRECTION INVERSION [main theory]; ECC [weapon index
    -- Haijima dependency]; FORMATION CAPTURE [weapon
    index -- Holy Sol theology]; MSC [measurement surface
    intact while function inverted]; EXTRACTION CYCLE
    [layered architecture as civilizational-scale
    extraction]; DWMA_SOUL_EATER [parallel institution
    -- correction instrument with extraction architecture
    running inside it].

[CASE:DWMA_SOUL_EATER]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
Death Weapon Meister Academy [Soul Eater]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Correction instrument as weapon factory
    [people cultivated as institutional instruments];
    GENUINE AXIS within captured institution [Maka/Soul
    partnership]; MEDUSA as high-CL deliberate extraction
    actor; person/tool boundary as extraction surface.

  CORE STRUCTURAL TENSION:
    DWMA's stated function: fight the Kishin, maintain
    sanity in the world, train meisters and weapons.
    Actual structural position: Shinigami's order requires
    weapons. Students are the weapons. The institution
    cultivates people as instruments of an existing power
    structure. The correction mandate [stop madness] and
    the extraction structure [produce weapons for the
    death god's order] run simultaneously inside the same
    institution.
    The meister/weapon partnership is the framework stress
    test: the partnership works when both sides have
    genuine axis and genuine correction capacity [Maka
    and Soul]. It fails into extraction when one side
    treats the other as pure instrument [Black Star's
    early arc; Crona/Ragnarok as the terminal failure
    case -- weapon fully captures meister].

  MAKA/SOUL AS GENUINE AXIS CONFIRMATION:
    The soul resonance mechanic is the operational
    definition of what the framework means by genuine
    partnership: both axes present, both correction
    capacities functional, the output exceeds what either
    produces alone. When one axis is captured or absent
    the resonance fails -- you can't fake soul resonance
    with a captured or absent axis. The series uses
    resonance as an empirical test for genuine vs
    performed partnership.
    Cross-domain confirmation: the same logic applies
    outside the fictional frame. Genuine collaboration
    produces outputs that neither party could produce
    alone. Performed collaboration produces neither
    party's full output. The resonance mechanic is
    fiction making the invisible visible.

  MEDUSA [high-CL deliberate extraction]:
    Genuine axis, genuine CAUSAL LEGIBILITY, full
    awareness of the extraction architecture, deliberate
    choice to accelerate it. Not captured -- she sees
    the system clearly and finds correction uninteresting.
    Specific failure mode: correct instrument, correct
    readings, axis aimed at extraction by choice not
    capture. This is the most dangerous configuration
    in the framework -- high CL + uncaptured axis +
    deliberate extraction means standard counter
    [restore legibility, break capture] doesn't work.
    The axis isn't captured. It's pointed the wrong way
    by design.
    Framework implication: not all extraction actors are
    captured. Some are legible and choosing. The
    intervention for captured axis is different from the
    intervention for deliberate extraction. Medusa
    distinguishes these.

  KISHIN [extraction to terminal state]:
    Asura as the endpoint of extraction consuming its
    own substrate -- fear consuming the capacity to
    experience anything other than fear. The madness
    wavelength is extraction architecture at biological
    substrate level: it converts the substrate [people's
    sanity and generative capacity] into the fuel for
    its own continuation. Terminal extraction confirmed.
    The series' answer [Maka's courage wavelength
    defeating madness] maps onto the framework's
    optimistic scenario: generation only needs to win
    once. Anti-coherence [madness] must win continuously.
    One correctable transmission [Maka reaching Asura's
    genuine fear underneath the madness] is sufficient
    to break the loop.

  Cross-ref: [REF:DWMA_SOUL_EATER];
    [CASE:COMPANY_3_FIRE_FORCE] [parallel institution --
    Okubo's recurring structure]; GENUINE AXIS [Maka/Soul
    resonance as empirical test]; MEDUSA [high-CL
    deliberate extraction -- see also IAGO, PALPATINE
    for same configuration]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST
    ASYMMETRY [Kishin confirmation -- madness must win
    continuously; correction wins once].

in the Type field of each stub below supersedes the untagged v16
Type field. No other changes to stub content.

  All purely fictional / game / anime cases: [S:1]
  Real-world with single-source or short window: [S:2]

  Multi-domain independent + controlled comparison: [S:4]
  Fictional cases cap at S:2 maximum.
  Cases with T8 recursion cap at S:2 regardless of evidence.

  criteria established in v16 [see classification protocol below].

CLASSIFICATION PROTOCOL [established v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
CONFIRMATION: independent domain + mechanism convergence + framework-
  independent falsifiability. All three required. Adds proof coverage.
ILLUSTRATION: framework vocabulary applied to real finding. Does not add
  proof coverage. Useful for teaching and extension. Cannot be cited as
  independent confirmation of framework claims.

Test for CONFIRMATION:
  [1] Independent domain: researcher had no knowledge of framework.
  [2] Mechanism convergence: mechanism matches framework prediction
      structurally, not just analogically.
  [3] Framework-independent falsifiability: finding has its own
      experimental confirmation pathway separate from framework.

If any criterion fails: ILLUSTRATION.
Both types are valuable. The distinction is what each can do.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

QUICK REFERENCE GLOSSARY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
QUALITY STACK [simple to complex]
  GENERATIVE CYCLE: system produces what it depends on to survive.
  SURPLUS PRODUCTION: system produces more than it consumes.
  CORRECTION CAPACITY: deviation detected and corrected.
  GENUINE AXIS: orientation internally generated and examined.
  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY: who counts as part of the measured population.
  PROPORTIONAL RESPONSE: correction doesn't become next failure.
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY: measurement surface not owned by extractor.
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY: what crosses the time boundary.
  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: harm traceable to actor who caused it.
  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY: successors can revise what they received.
  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING: axis traceable to actual outputs.
  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION: active restoration of what GC runs on.
  SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION: active release of surplus outward.
  TENSION MAINTENANCE: sustains productive tension. Failure: Collapse.
  TENSION DISSOLUTION: drive toward false resolution. Failure: False
    Harmony.

WEAPON COMPRESSIONS
  RaQM  Rational Quantum Mechanics [Palmer -- discrete rational
         substrate; falsifiable ~2029 via qubit wall at ~400-1000].
  ECD   Engineered Causal Diffusion [MI -- chain illegibility].
  MSC   Measurement Surface Control [CL -- owns the instrument].
  CS    Calibration Strip [TS -- locks the reference standard].
  AGC   Axis Gestation Capture [SA -- contaminates at formation].
  CT    Correction Theatre [CC -- performance not function].
  FL    Fertile Lock [TS -- locks transmission permanently].
  ECC   Exit Cost Construction [GC -- raises cost of leaving].
  DC    Dependency Construction [GC -- requires ongoing supply].
  BM    Boundary Manipulation [CC -- shifts who counts].
  CM    Coherence Mimicry [TS -- appears coherent, isn't].
  FC    Formation Capture [SA -- captures at formation window].
  PC    Proxy Capture [SA/CAG -- harvests output of genuine system].

KEY FINDINGS
  True Coherence: generates more than it consumes. Self-sustaining.
  False Coherence: consumes to maintain appearance of generating.
  Gift: gives coherence. Weapon: takes coherence away.
  Ascending path: hypothesized single sustainable direction. PROVISIONAL.
  Structural Correctness: correct behavior falsifiable by outputs.
  Section Two-B: speculative only. Do not use for scoring.
  Biological baseline: GC + SP + CC confirmed at every scale cell to
    person.
  SA Suppression: weapon stack prevents SA development at population
    scale. Case library overweighted toward outliers.

BIOLOGICAL / ECOLOGICAL CONFIRMATIONS AND ILLUSTRATIONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Elephant Stack [strongest non-human CAUSAL MEMORY case in library]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: CAUSAL MEMORY + CYCLE STABILITY +
  GENERATIVE CYCLE confirmed at non-human substrate]
GC/SP/CC/BOUNDARY INTEGRITY/CL/CYCLE STABILITY/CAUSAL MEMORY confirmed.
Proto-SA [mirror self-recognition].
CAUSAL MEMORY: matriarchs retain correctable environmental history
  across decades. Herds without experienced matriarchs make measurably
  worse decisions under novel threat. CM failure = outcome difference
  directly measurable. CYCLE STABILITY [Relational Bonding]: 50-year
  relationships confirmed, grief behavior. No TS [non-contact generations
  cannot transmit].

Themis-Grb2 complex [VIB/Ghent/VUB, Nature Communications 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: CYCLE STABILITY as relational bonding +
  TENSION MAINTENANCE at molecular scale + CAUSAL LEGIBILITY instrument
  upgrade pattern]
Two proteins in sustained reciprocal structural relationship neither
  can perform alone. Themis extensively wraps around Grb2 [multiple
  cooperative contact points, stable constitutive complex].
TENSION MAINTENANCE confirmed at protein scale: Grb2 stable enough
  to hold complex, flexible enough to recruit additional signaling
  proteins on T cell activation. Destroy either property [stability
  or flexibility] and T cell development fails.
CAUSAL LEGIBILITY confirmation: 20 years of knowing Themis was
  essential [MI surface intact] without causal mechanism visible.
  Cryo-EM made structural chain legible -- CL restored by better
  instrument. Pattern confirmed independently: Halocynthia papillosa
  sea squirt [RUB, Communications Biology 2026] -- CNS assumed standard
  structure for decades, synchrotron tomography revealed fundamentally
  different anatomy. Same mechanism: surface intact, CL absent,
  instrument upgrade restores legibility.
Applications: autoimmune disease, immune deficiency treatment pathways.

Nanotube DNA transfer [UT Southwestern CRI, Cell 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: TS failure via INPUT FILTERING breach at
  genomic scale; Calibration Strip at genetic substrate]
Horizontal genomic DNA transfer between somatic cells via nanotubes,
  previously considered impossible. Large fragments transferred,
  incorporated into recipient genome, remain biologically active and
  alter cell behavior.
TS mechanism: what crosses the nanotube is damaged/corrupted DNA --
  locked state transmitted, not correctable axis. Calibration Strip
  at genomic level: corrupted reference material alters recipient's
  GC substrate directly.
INPUT FILTERING failure: recipient cell's input screening does not
  reject foreign DNA arriving via nanotube. Garbage Cycle -- GC runs
  correctly on corrupted substrate because IF fails at the nanotube
  boundary.
Cancer application: tumors using nanotube transfer to spread mutations
  to healthy cells = weaponized TS. Horizontal transmission of
  extraction capacity. Confirmed across different cell types.

Genetic architecture tails [Nature, May 2026 -- Souaiaia et al.]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: REFERENCE INTEGRITY failure at genomic
  measurement scale; TENSION MAINTENANCE at population scale]
Polygenic risk scores [PRS] built on common-variant architecture have
  lower predictive accuracy at trait tails because the measurement
  surface is calibrated for the middle of the distribution.
  Architecture genuinely differs at extremes; instrument does not.
  Calibration Strip: reference standard built on majority architecture,
  applied to tail population where it fails.
INPUT FILTERING at methodological level: infinitesimal model
  [all variants small effect, additive] structurally filters out tail
  architecture.
TENSION MAINTENANCE as biological mechanism: stabilizing selection
  maintains the distribution's coherence. Extreme values penalized
  [reduced fecundity confirmed]; large-effect alleles kept rare.
  TENSION MAINTENANCE operating correctly produces heterogeneous tail
  architecture as byproduct. Replicated across ancestries, cohorts,
  repeated measures. 74 traits.

Glymphatic system [University of Rochester, Science Advances 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: SUBSTRATE REGENERATION gated by CYCLE
  STABILITY; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY instrument upgrade pattern]
SR passive/active hybrid confirmed at neurological scale. Passive SR
  = baseline CSF circulation. Active SR = deep sleep trigger [CYCLE

  MRI velocity data -- previously unmeasurable.
CYCLE STABILITY as gating mechanism: disrupted sleep disrupts active
  SR window. Explains why irregular sleep is worse than consistently
  short sleep for neurodegeneration risk -- erratic CS prevents active
  SR from firing, not just reducing its duration.
CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: glymphatic system described 2012; flow velocity
  unquantifiable until AI instrument. Same pattern as Themis-Grb2 --
  surface intact, CL absent, instrument upgrade restores legibility.
Alzheimer's/neurodegeneration: SR hybrid failure -- passive SR
  insufficient without active SR; amyloid-beta/tau accumulation =
  substrate atrophy through missed clearance windows.

Neutral Theory of Evolution challenge [University of Michigan, May 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: REFERENCE INTEGRITY failure at calibration
  standard scale; TENSION MAINTENANCE at genomic scale]
Neutral theory [Kimura 1968] served as calibration anchor for
  population genetics, phylogenetic timing, drug resistance modeling,
  and speciation analysis. Study finds beneficial mutations significantly
  more common than neutral theory predicts -- selection doing more work
  than reference standard assigned.
MI can be locally intact while RI fails globally. Classic Reference
  Drift: surface honest to wrong baseline. Every downstream instrument
  inherits miscalibration without detecting it.
CL failure embedded in prior model: fitness effects treated as
  intrinsic properties of mutations. Study confirms they are relational
  -- mutation-environment pairs, not mutations alone.
TENSION MAINTENANCE at genomic scale: "a mutation helpful in one
  setting may carry costs in others" -- no fixed fitness value, only
  relational fitness held in environmental tension.
Reuse finding [University of York, May 2026]: evolution reuses same
  genes across 120 million years, modifying regulatory switches not
  gene sequences. CAG confirmation -- evolutionary axis anchored in
  constrained solution space, not open random walk.
Limitation: primary data from yeast and E. coli. BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
  note declared.

Ousiometrics / semantic axes [University of Vermont, Science Advances,
  May 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: REFERENCE INTEGRITY failure at

REFERENCE INTEGRITY failure at measurement-standard scale.

Framework parallel [significant]: PDS axes map directly to framework
  qualities. Power = CC [who can initiate change]. Danger = threat to
  GC or SR. Structure = CAG [relational position within organized
  system]. If meaning organizes around these axes at semantic
  foundation, language is encoding coherence-relevant information not
  primarily feeling-states.
Implication: language did not evolve to tag emotions -- it evolved
  to tag relational position within generative systems. Supports
  framework claim that coherence qualities are pre-existing structure
  that functioning systems align with, not human constructs.
Source: Dodds et al., Science Advances [May 6 2026].

Willpower depletion / attention capture [Stanford / Neuroscience News,

[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: Intermittent Reinforcement as CYCLE

Smartphones and communication platforms intentionally exploit dopamine-

  breach. Proactive control = BI established before stimulus arrives;

  than repeated CC firing.
SR implication: cognitive depletion is literal substrate atrophy.
  Willpower failure is not character failure -- it is SR failure under
  sustained CC demand.

Thalamic consciousness signature [LMU Munich, Nature Human Behaviour,
  May 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: CYCLE STABILITY as binary gate confirmed
  at consciousness substrate]
20-45 Hz oscillation in central human thalamus fires exclusively
  during conscious waking and REM sleep; falls completely silent
  during non-REM. Direct intracranial recordings via deep brain
  stimulation electrodes. Gate-vs-gradient distinction: consciousness
  is not reduced during non-REM, it is absent. Oscillation is not a
  continuous variable -- it is a threshold. CYCLE STABILITY as binary
  gate rather than continuous function.
MI/CL split: consciousness known to exist [surface intact]; thalamic
  mechanism illegible until direct electrode access. Instrument upgrade
  restores legibility. Framework prediction confirmed: MI/CL split

  was traceable.
Clinical implication: oscillation as measurable biological signature
  could optimize deep brain stimulation approaches.
Source: Staudigl et al., LMU Munich [May 2026].

Chromatin aging / SIRT6 restoration [Bar-Ilan University, Nature
  Communications, May 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: REFERENCE INTEGRITY drift and reset at

Distinct from DNA damage [nanotube case] -- underlying DNA intact.

Tardigrade cryptobiosis [suspension without decay]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: CYCLE STABILITY as binary gate, three-
  state property confirmed]
Gate can remain closed indefinitely [decades documented] with full
  restoration on water return. Substrate consumption and cycle
  operation decouple when gate closes completely. Vitrification
  [glass-like state] = structural preservation without function.
Framework implication: CYCLE STABILITY gate has three states not two
  [open / reduced / fully closed]. Fully closed is not the worst state
  -- it is the most stable under external threat. GC at zero is
  survivable when SR is also at zero. The danger is partial states
  where cycle reduced but substrate still consumed.

Turritopsis dohrnii [immortal jellyfish -- reversible developmental
  gate]
[ILLUSTRATION -- CYCLE STABILITY gate fourth property]
Tardigrade established three-state gate [open / reduced / fully
  closed]. Jellyfish adds: gate position reversible across
  developmental stages. Under stress, reverts to polyp stage -- not
  paused adult but prior developmental state.
Framework implication: CS gate not unidirectional. Can reverse
  developmental trajectory under sufficient substrate threat.
  Two gate reversal mechanisms confirmed: tardigrade = suspend and
  restore; jellyfish = revert and rebuild.

Compressed sensing [Candès et al. -- Shaw Prize 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY restoration at
  measurement instrument level; MINIMAL UNIFICATION]
Accurate signal reconstruction from incomplete data -- the chain
  [full signal] becomes legible from partial surface [sparse
  measurements]. Prior assumption: full signal requires full sampling.
  Compressed sensing proves chain reconstructible from far fewer
  measurements when signal has structure.
Medical imaging application: MRI scan times reduced because fewer
  measurements needed for diagnostic-quality reconstruction.
Framework implication: CL gaps are not always fixed by more
  measurement -- sometimes by smarter instrument design. Same data,
  different instrument, chain becomes legible.
Source: Shaw Prize 2026.

Non-Mendelian epigenetic inheritance [Johns Hopkins / Nature Genetics,
  May 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: TS at epigenetic scale; REFERENCE
  INTEGRITY incomplete calibration standard]
Mendel's laws calibration standard for 150 years. Still accurate for
  93% of inheritance patterns. But 7% of epigenetic methylation marks
  [522 cases across three mouse generations] behave unexpectedly --
  reference standard was incomplete, not wrong.
TS finding: environmental experience transmits across generations via
  epigenetic marks, not DNA sequence. First naturally occurring
  paramutation confirmed in mammals. Information about environmental
  conditions crosses the generational transmission boundary.
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY operates at epigenetic scale -- what crosses
  the generational boundary is not just genetic sequence but methylation
  state encoding environmental history.
Extends chromatin/aging entry: same methylation mechanism, different
  scale.
Source: Davidovich et al., Nature Genetics [May 2026].

Sea anemone transgenerational epigenetic inheritance [Queen Mary
  University of London / Xu et al., Nature Ecology & Evolution,
  June 2026]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY at epigenetic
  scale confirmed in second independent animal system; TS three-tier
  structure; methylation ancestral role as genome protection not
  gene regulation]
Researchers experimentally removed DNA methylation in sea anemone
  [Nematostella vectensis]. Animals developed normally despite losing
  most methylation. Rather than causing gene regulation defects,
  methylation loss mainly unleashed transposable elements -- "jumping
  genes" embedded within active genes that can insert into important
  regulatory regions and disrupt development.
Because sea anemones lack the extensive epigenetic resetting after
  fertilization that mammals have, experimentally induced methylation
  changes persisted in offspring and altered gene expression --
  transgenerational epigenetic inheritance confirmed experimentally
  in an animal lineage.
TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY three-tier finding [new -- v17]:
  Prior entries [Davidovich mouse paramutation; nanotube DNA] confirm
  two TS tiers: genetic sequence [what crosses by default] and
  methylation state encoding environmental history [what sometimes
  crosses in mammals]. This entry adds a third:
  Tier 1: genetic sequence -- crosses by default across all animals.
  Tier 2: methylation state encoding environmental history --
    crosses in invertebrates by default; crosses in mammals only
    under specific conditions [paramutation, nanotube].
  Tier 3: methylation state as genome stability mechanism --
    suppresses transposable elements; its ancestral function is
    blocking genomic parasites from propagating, not regulating
    gene expression. Weapon and defense use the same mechanism.
  Implication: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY is not a single gate.
    It is a layered system with different reset thresholds at each
    tier. What crosses the generational boundary is determined by
    which tier is active and what the reset mechanism does to it.
REFERENCE INTEGRITY extension: methylation's role was calibrated
  as "gene regulation" by mammalian-centered research. Sea anemone
  finding recalibrates: primary ancestral function is genome
  protection. Mammalian gene regulation via methylation is a
  derived secondary function built on top of the protective
  substrate. Every downstream model assuming gene regulation as
  primary function inherits the miscalibration silently.
Confirmation criteria met: [1] independent domain [evolutionary
  epigenomics, no framework knowledge], [2] mechanism convergence
  [TS operating at epigenetic scale in second independent animal
  system, distinct mechanism from Davidovich], [3] framework-
  independent falsifiability [Nature Ecology & Evolution experimental
  design with methylation knockout].
Classification: CONFIRMATION [TS three-tier structure; second
  independent animal confirmation; RI recalibration for methylation
  primary function].
Source: Xu et al. [Nature Ecology & Evolution, June 2026]; Queen
  Mary University of London [June 2026]; Phys.org [June 7 2026].

Entangled quantum states from minimal differentiation [Chu et al.,

Gold hydride [Frost et al., SLAC/European XFEL, Angew. Chem.,

[ILLUSTRATION -- REFERENCE INTEGRITY; calibration standard reactive

Gold chosen as passive reference material in high-pressure hydrocarbon

  standards require testing across full range of conditions].

Two independent domains [immunology + scientific publishing] arriving

Germinal center B cell selection [Victora Lab / Rockefeller, Cell,

[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: distributed iterative CC outperforms

Confirmation criteria met: [1] independent domain, [2] mechanism

  centralized correction inferior in complex high-chaos environments].

Supergiant isopod starvation tolerance [Yuan et al., Cell, June 2026]
[ILLUSTRATION -- SUBSTRATE REGENERATION + IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT]
Bathynomus deep-sea isopods survive 5+ years without food via two-

  intake at feast events] + extremely low basal metabolic rate
  [minimum ongoing expenditure between feast events]. Horizontal gene
  transfer from bacteria [ND1 gene] acts as energy-saving switch.
Framework read: C1 adapted to extreme substrate scarcity by decoupling
  intake capacity from metabolic demand. Maximum intake burst +
  minimum ongoing expenditure = survival across 5+ year idle cycles.
IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT confirmation: organism's survival architecture
  treats idle cycles as load-bearing, not as waste.
Scale Recurrence note: ND1 energy-saving mechanism horizontally
  acquired from bacteria -- same mechanism at different substrate
  scales. Confirms IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT is not culturally
  constructed; it is a structural property of biological systems
  under resource scarcity.
Classification: ILLUSTRATION [framework vocabulary applied; does not
  add proof coverage beyond existing cellular minimum confirmation].
Source: Yuan et al. [Cell, June 2026].

ECOLOGICAL EXTRACTION CASES [full entries]
