Cuckoo brood parasitism [obligate interspecific brood parasite]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: Formation Capture + Exit Cost Construction
  + Calibration Strip co-deployed]
Cuckoo deposits egg in host nest during brief absence. Host incubates
  egg without detecting substitution. On hatching, cuckoo chick
  executes three-weapon stack independently of adult:
  Formation Capture: chick receives parental investment at
    developmental window before host offspring can form independent
    relationship with parent. Host axis for "offspring" forms on
    cuckoo substrate.
  Exit Cost Construction: chick ejects host eggs and nestlings
    immediately after hatching -- eliminates alternative substrate
    sources. Host cannot distribute investment to genuine offspring;
    all investment routes to cuckoo.
  Calibration Strip: cuckoo chick produces supernormal stimuli
    [exaggerated gape, call frequency, color] calibrated above host
    offspring's range. Host calibration standard for "offspring in
    need" overwrites to cuckoo's signal. Host feeds cuckoo
    preferentially even when cuckoo is visibly larger than host.
    Classic Reference Drift.
Mechanism confirmed by behavioral ecology [Davies, Brooke -- Nature
  1988, 1989; Dawkins/Krebs arms race model] without framework
  vocabulary. Supernormal stimulus mechanism [Tinbergen] independently
  confirmed. ECC [egg ejection] documented across 100+ host species.
Framework implication: Formation Capture + ECC + Calibration Strip
  co-deployment is not uniquely human. Structural weapon stack emerges
  independently in biological systems under selection pressure.
  Confirms: weapon mechanisms are load-bearing structural patterns,
  not cultural inventions.
Source: Davies & Brooke [Nature 1988, 1989]; Tinbergen [1951].

Ophiocordyceps [zombie-ant fungus -- Ophiocordyceps unilateralis]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: GENUINE AXIS capture at neural substrate;
  Axis Substitution at biological minimum]
Fungal parasite infects carpenter ant [Camponotus leonardi]. Fungus
  infiltrates brain, overrides locomotion control, compels ant to
  climb to precise height [25cm], bite leaf vein at solar noon, then
  die. Fungus fruits through ant's head at optimal spore dispersal
  position. Host C1 intact [ant metabolizes, moves, grips] but GA
  fully substituted -- behavior serves fungal reproductive cycle not
  ant survival.
Neurobiological confirmation [de Bekker et al., 2015, BMC Genomics]:
  fungus produces behavior-altering compounds targeting specific brain
  regions. Host locomotion hijacked without destroying host substrate
  -- substrate maintained alive to execute fungal axis.
Framework implication: GA capture at neural substrate level confirmed
  in non-human system. Axis substitution is a structural biological
  mechanism, not a metaphor. Extracting system maintains host C1
  precisely because extraction requires it -- consumption dominance
  would defeat the weapon. Confirms Smart Extraction principle at
  biological minimum.
Source: de Bekker et al. [BMC Genomics, 2015]; Hughes et al. [2011].

Cleaner fish deception [Labroides dimidiatus -- bluestreak cleaner
  wrasse]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: Measurement Surface Control + Correction
  Theatre co-deployed; C2 capture at ecological scale]
Cleaner wrasse operate cleaning stations where client fish present
  for ectoparasite removal. Cleaner has two strategies: genuine
  cleaning [cooperative] and cheating [biting client mucus --
  preferred food, higher caloric value than parasites].
Bshary et al. [2002, Nature] documented systematic cheating
  modulation based on audience composition:
  Client alone: cheating rate higher [no reputation cost].
  Bystander present: cheating rate lower [future client deterred].
  Inspector fish present: cheating suppressed entirely [CC elevated].
Mechanism: cleaner controls client's measurement surface by
  modulating behavior based on C2 instrument availability.
Correction Theatre confirmed: visible cleaning activity performed;
  actual cheating rate a function of observation capacity not
  cooperative intent.
MSC at ecological scale: cleaner reads which C2 instruments are
  present and adjusts deception accordingly. Identical to institutional
  MSC [behavior honest when audited, extractive otherwise].
Framework implication: C2 surface control and Correction Theatre
  co-deployment are not uniquely human. Extraction adjusts to
  available correction capacity as a default biological behavior.
Source: Bshary & Würth [2001]; Bshary et al. [2002, Nature].

ECOLOGICAL EXTRACTION SUMMARY:
Three independent biological systems confirm three distinct weapon
  mechanisms without human cultural substrate:
  Cuckoo: FC + ECC + CS [co-deployed stack; weapons emerge under
    selection pressure without design]
  Ophiocordyceps: GA substitution [axis capture at biological minimum;
    host C1 maintained for extraction]
  Cleaner wrasse: MSC + CT [C2 surface modulated by CC availability;
    not uniquely human]
Combined implication: extraction mechanisms are structural properties
  of biological systems under selection pressure. Framework is not
  describing human cultural pathology -- it is describing load-bearing
  patterns that emerge independently across substrates. Strongest
  cross-domain confirmation class in appendix.

Anderson et al. [Psychological Bulletin 2015 + Psychological Science 2012]
[CONFIRMATION -- L3.5 SR correlation; sociometric vs socioeconomic
  status distinction; genuine contribution as wellbeing mechanism]
Status defined as "the respect, admiration, and voluntary deference
  individuals are afforded by others" [Anderson, Hildreth, Howland,
  UC Berkeley 2015] -- explicitly separated from power, financial
  success, and need to belong. Sociometric status [respect within
  face-to-face groups] tracked subjective wellbeing more closely
  than socioeconomic status [2012].
Framework confirmation: L3.5 RECOGNITION in genuine face-to-face
  groups is the load-bearing wellbeing variable, not positional rank.
  Sociometric status is a byproduct of actual contribution to people
  you know -- it tracks whether SP is occurring in the immediate
  relational environment. Socioeconomic status is positional and
  can decouple from generative function entirely.
  Confirms framework prediction: axis oriented toward genuine
  contribution in real relational contexts [L3.5 functioning]
  outperforms axis oriented toward positional extraction.
  The drive for recognition-in-real-groups is deep and basic --
  which simultaneously confirms L3.5 as load-bearing substrate
  AND identifies it as the primary target for extraction
  architecture [synthetic recognition at scale harvests the
  same drive without completing the transfer].
Classification: CONFIRMATION [external E3 evidence for L3.5 SR
  correlation; framework prediction confirmed from outside].
Source: Anderson et al. Psychological Bulletin [2015];
  Anderson Psychological Science [2012]. Space Daily [June 2026].

Baumeister et al. [Journal of Positive Psychology 2013]
[CONFIRMATION -- SP/SR divergence; happiness vs meaning as
  distinct cycle components; giving as meaning mechanism;
  stress as generative signal]
Four researchers [Roy Baumeister et al.] separated happiness
  from meaning empirically. Key findings:
  [1] Happiness tracks receiving [benefits one receives from
    others]; meaning tracks giving [benefits others receive
    from the self]. Clear and strong finding: givers have
    more meaningful lives than takers.
  [2] Higher levels of worry, stress, and anxiety were linked
    to higher meaningfulness but lower happiness. Generative
    stress produces meaning; comfort produces happiness.
  [3] Connecting past and present [temporal coherence,
    identity continuity] linked to meaning not happiness.
  Framework confirmation: SP and SR are distinct cycle
    components operating on different axes. Happiness [hedonia]
    tracks SR -- substrate being replenished. Meaning [eudaimonia]
    tracks SP -- surplus generated beyond self-maintenance cost.
    The divergence is structurally predicted: a well-maintained
    instrument [high SR] generating no surplus [low SP] is stable
    but non-generative. Generative stress [high SP effort] produces
    meaning precisely because the cycle is running.
  EXTRACTION IMPLICATION [confirmed]:
    ICD and synthetic comfort products deliver hedonic component
    [SR signal, happiness] without eudaimonic component [SP
    output, meaning]. The instrument feels maintained while the
    generative cycle degrades. The SR signal feels like the
    whole thing; the SP absence is not immediately detectable.
    This is more precise than "AI companionship is bad" --
    it delivers exactly the hedonic component while
    systematically failing to deliver the eudaimonic component.
    Knowing deployment confirms: the divergence is documented
    in their own research; they know what the product delivers
    and what it doesn't.
Classification: CONFIRMATION [E3 -- published peer-reviewed
  study; mechanism consistent with framework prediction;
  giving/stress/meaning relationship confirmed].
Source: Baumeister et al. Journal of Positive Psychology [2013].
  Space Daily [June 2026].
Cross-ref: SURPLUS PRODUCTION [quality definition -- giving
  as SP output]; SUBSTRATE REGENERATION [happiness as SR
  signal]; ICD [weapon index -- SR delivery without SP;
  SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD [knowing deployment -- hedonic/
  eudaimonic divergence documented internally].

OPEN QUESTIONS / PROGRESS TRACKING
==========================================================================

Big G [gravitational constant] -- status: UNRESOLVED [tracking]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  G = 6.674 x 10^-11 N⋅m²/kg². Least precisely measured fundamental
  constant in physics. 22 parts per million uncertainty -- orders of
  magnitude worse than Planck's constant or speed of light.
  16+ independent measurements since 1980s. Values scattered and
  mutually inconsistent. April 2026 Schlamminger replication [13 tons
  mercury, decade-long experiment] produced different value from the
  result it attempted to replicate.

  Why measurement is hard:
    Gravity is the weakest force. Signal from lab masses tiny.
    Every surrounding object contributes gravitational noise.
    No way to shield against gravity [unlike EM]. Environmental
    contamination irreducible with current methods.

  Three hypotheses worth tracking:
    H1 [standard]: G is a true constant; scatter reflects
      experimental systematics. Eventually precision improves and
      values converge. Prediction: future replications converge.
      Current status: not happening after 40 years.
    H2 [emergent/secondary]: G is not fundamental but emergent
      from something deeper [Verlinde entropic gravity; G
      derivable from thermodynamic/statistical properties].
      If true: lab-scale scatter expected because you're measuring
      an emergent statistical quantity sensitive to local mass
      configurations. Astronomical scale averages out; lab scale
      doesn't. Prediction: scatter doesn't converge regardless of
      precision improvement.
    H3 [scale-dependent]: gravitational behavior varies at extreme
      scales [MOND territory -- very low acceleration regimes].
      Prediction: lab G and cosmological G measurably different.

  Framework relevance:
    Physics constants as substrate floor [main theory]: if G is
      emergent [H2], the "tower to physics" argument changes.
      G wouldn't be a free parameter or a designed constant --
      it would be a downstream consequence of more fundamental
      structure. Design inference weakens on G specifically; may
      strengthen on whatever G emerges from.
    REFERENCE INTEGRITY: Big G is a measurement surface that
      resists precise calibration. If H2 correct, every
      calculation using G as constant has a soft floor.

  Track: does scatter converge [H1 supported] or persist despite
    precision improvement [H2/H3 supported]?
    Current trajectory: scatter persisting. H1 weakening.

  FUTURE DATA SOURCE [Hubble anomaly catalogue -- v16]:
    AnomalyMatch [O'Ryan/Gómez, ESA, Jan 2026] catalogued 1,300+
    anomalous objects from 35-year Hubble archive, 800+ previously
    undocumented. Several dozen defy existing classification entirely.
    Potential relevance: gravitational lensing geometry at unusual
    mass ratios may constrain G behavior at scales between lab and
    astronomical. Unclassifiables are highest-priority -- anomalies
    not fitting current models are where theoretical revision
    historically originates.
    Status: catalogue available; analysis against alternative
    gravity frameworks not yet run. Flag for future work.

  Source: Live Science June 2026; Schlamminger/NIST April 2026;
    Rothleitner/PTB commentary; Verlinde [2010].

  COHERENT ALTERNATIVE FRAMEWORK [Timescape + Verlinde]:
    Timescape cosmology [Wiltshire]: apparent expansion acceleration
      is a geometric averaging artifact. Inhomogeneous spacetime means
      regions with different mass densities have different local clock
      rates. What looks like acceleration is a measurement artifact
      from averaging across these regions, not a real force. No dark
      energy substance required.
    Verlinde entropic gravity: gravity emerges from entropy gradients
      in an information-theoretic substrate. G is a statistical
      quantity, not a fundamental constant -- emergent from underlying
      structure sensitive to local mass/information distribution.
    Why they cohere: both treat spacetime geometry as emergent or
      statistical rather than fundamental. Both explain anomalous
      observations [expansion, galactic rotation] without new
      substances. Combined prediction: G varies slightly based on
      local entropy/information density. Big G measurement scatter
      = signal not noise.
    Honest limits: Verlinde has problems with precision gravitational
      lensing tests. Timescape hasn't matched ΛCDM in predictive
      precision. Coherent as framework; not established as physics.
      Track against H2 above.

  BRANS-DICKE SCALAR-TENSOR THEORY [tracking -- v16]:
    G coupled to a scalar field, varying slowly over cosmological
    time. Addresses Big G variability as feature not bug -- G isn't
    constant because it's genuinely coupled to cosmological evolution.
    Fits Timescape + Verlinde picture: all three treat G as non-
    fundamental. Brans-Dicke provides the cosmological mechanism;
    Verlinde provides the microscale entropic substrate; Timescape
    handles expansion artifact.
    Constrained by solar system precision tests [coupling parameter
    ω must be large, reducing to GR locally] but not ruled out.
    Large ω limit recovers standard GR at solar system scales while
    allowing cosmological variation.
    Track: does improved cosmological G measurement show slow drift
    consistent with scalar-tensor coupling? Any confirmed G variation
    over cosmological time would strongly support this framework.
    Source: Brans & Dicke [1961]; subsequent constraint literature;
    Cassini spacecraft ω > 40,000 bound.
